irupattiraṇṭu curutikaḷ



innūluḷ . . ¯
iraṇṭām pākam : itu oru stāyiyil vaḻaṅkum
curam curutikaḷippaṟṟip palar collum apippirāyaṅ
kaḷi ottuppārttu avikaḷ taṟkāla aṉupōkattiṟku
ottu varātaviyeṉṟu teḷivuṟak kāṭṭum.



















karuṇāmirta cākaram
mutal puttakam - iraṇṭām pākam
poruḷ aṭṭavaṇi
   
kaṭavuḷ tuṇi.

karuṇāmirta cākaram.
mutal puttakam.
iraṇṭām pākam.
irupattiraṇṭu curutikaḷ.
ciṟappuṟṟōṅkiya nam intiyāvil curutikaḷip paṟṟic collum ovvoruvarum cāraṅkatēvar colliya tuvāvimcati curutikaḷ 22 ivitāṉ eṉṟum, ivikaḷāyirukkilāmeṉṟum, ivikaḷāyirukku meṉṟu nām eṇṇalāmeṉṟum, nām niccayikkalāmeṉṟum colva tiṉālum, teṉṉintiya caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷil cilarum teṉṉintiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷ 22 eṉṟum, cāraṅkaruṭiya apip pirāyam itutāṉ eṉṟum colvatiṉālum, mutal mutal tuvāvimcati curutikaḷip paṟṟi maṟṟavar collum apippirāyaṅkaḷiyum cāraṅkar tuvāvimcati curutiyippaṟṟic collum apippirāyattiyum ārāycci ceytu, avikaḷiṉ muṭivu terintu koṇṭu ataṉpiṉ karnāṭaka caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷip pārkka vēṇṭum.
cāraṅkatēvarip paṟṟiyum curutiyip paṟṟi avar colliya
cūttiraṅkaḷip paṟṟiyum cila kuṟippukaḷ
caṅkīta ratṉākaram eḻutiṉavar cāraṅkatēvar. ivar kāsmīra (Kashmir) tēcattil piṟantavar. varuṇakaṇariṣi kulattavar. ki.pi. 1210-m varuṣa mutal 1247-m varuṣam vari tulatapāt (near Aurangabad in Hyderabad) allatu tēvakiriyil āṇṭu koṇṭirunta cimmaṇarājaṉ (cōmarāja makārājaṉ) kālattiliruntavar. intac cōma rājāviṉuṭiya kēṭṭuk koḷḷutaluk  kiṇaṅki innūl ceytatākat tōṉṟukiṟatu. ivar tam kālattil vaḻaṅki vanta caṅkītattukkuriya amcaṅkaḷ yāviyum  camas kirutattil veku naṉṟāka eḻuti irukkiṟār. innūlil curutikaḷip paṟṟit teriya virumpum yāvarum aṟiya vēṇṭiya viparam collap paṭuvatiṉālum, itu paḻamiyāka eṇṇappaṭuvatiṉālum, curutikaḷip paṟṟi ivar eṉṉa collukiṟār eṉṟu terintu koḷvatu mutal kaṭamiyākum. ivar ceyta caṅkītaratṉākarattil cura attiyāyattil collappaṭum cila cūttiraṅkaḷiṉ karutti uḷḷatu uḷḷapaṭiyē moḻi peyarttu iṅku eḻutukiṟēṉ.
“iraṇṭu vīṇi tayār ceytu koḷ. ovvoṉṟukkum 22 tantikaḷ pōṭu. atil oru vīṇiyil mutal tantiyil uṉṉāl kūṭiya ārampa nātam varumpaṭivi. ataṉ kīḻ vēṟu nātamirukkakkūṭātu. ataṉmēl koñcam kūṭutalāka 
2-m tantiyi amittukkoḷ. iraṇṭu tantikkum naṭu mattiyil vēṟu nātam uṇṭākātapaṭiyirukkaṭṭum. itē pirakāramāka oṉṟiṉ mēloṉṟāy curuti cērttukkoḷ. ippaṭi mēl tantikaḷ pōkappōka oṉṟukkoṉṟu tīviramākum. ippaṭi iraṇṭum tayārceytukoḷ. atil ṣaṭjamam 4 curuti koṇṭatu. itil nālāvatu curutiyi ṣaṭjamamāka vittuk koḷ. riṣapattiṟku 3 curuti, 5-vatu 6-vatu 7-vatu tantikaḷil riṣapam niṟkum. kāntārattiṟku 2 curuti, 8-vatu 9-vatu tantikaḷil varum. mattimattiṟku 4 curuti, 10, 11, 12, 13-vatu tantikaḷil niṟkum. pañcamattiṟku 4 curuti, 14, 15, 16, 17-vatu tantikaḷil toṉikkum. tivatattiṟku 3 curuti, 18, 19, 20-vatu tantikaḷil pēcum. niṣātattiṟku 2 curuti, atuvum 21, 22-l muṭikiṟatu. ivaṟṟil oṉṟu turuva vīṇi, maṟṟoṉṟu cala vīṇi eṉṟu vittuk koḷ. ati cala vīṇiyi nāṉ collukiṟapaṭi māṟṟu. 4-vatu ṣaṭjamattiṉ  piṉṉuḷḷa ṣaṭjamattiṉ 3-vatu curutiyiliruntu muṉ kiramappaṭi capta curaṅkaḷi vittāl, oru curuti kuṟiyum. iraṇṭāvatu 2 curuti kuṟittuk koṇṭupōka ka vum ni yum riṣapa tivatattiṉ curutikaḷil oṉṟi aṭiyum. mūṉṟāvatu 3 curuti kuṟittuk koṇṭu pōka riṣapa tivatam ṣaṭjama pañcamattiṉ 4 curutiyip peṟum. 4 curuti māṟṟumpōtu turuva vīṇiyiluḷḷa ni, ka, ma vil cala vīṇiyiṉ ca, ma, pa layatti aṭikiṟatu. atāvatu 22-l ṣaṭjamamum 9-l mattimamum, 13-l pañcamamum ārampikkum. inta nāluvitam curuti kuṟippatiṉāl turuva vīṇiyiluḷḷa curaṅkaḷiṉ layattiyaṭikiṟatu. itiṉāl curaṅkaḷiṉ kaṇakku aṟiyappaṭum. ippaṭippaṭṭa curutikaḷilirun tum ṣaṭjam, riṣapam, kāntāram, mattimam, pañcamam, tivatam, niṣātam eṉṉum ēḻu curaṅkaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa.”
ivviṭattil caṅkīta ratṉākararuṭiya cūttiraṅkaḷi avaṟṟiluḷḷa paṭiyē arttam ceytirukkum kiḷameṇṭs (Mr. Clements) avarkaḷiṉ cila vākkiyaṅkaḷiyum iṅkē eḻutuvatu nallateṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu.
caṅkīta ratṉākarar tuvāvimcati curutikaḷip paṟṟic colliya cila cūttiraṅkaḷukku Mr.. kiḷameṇṭs I.C.S. eḻutiya arttattiṉ moḻi peyarppu.
Indroduction to the Study of Indian Music by E. Clements P. 53.
THE SANGIT RATNAKAR
“Take two Vinas with 22 wires each and tune as follows. Let the first wire give the lowest possible note. The next a note a little higher and so on, so that between the notes given by any two adjacent wires a third note is impossible.
These successive notes are the Srutis. Sa will stand on the fourth wire, being a svara of four Srutis; ri will be on the third wire counting from the fifth; ga which has only two Srutis will fall on the second, counting from the eighth; ma being of four Srutis on the fourth, counting from the tenth; Pa on the fourth, counting from the fourteenth; Dha on the third after pa; ni on the second after Dha; so Ni will fall on the twenty-second Sruti.”
intiya caṅkītattiṟku mukavuriyāka kiḷameṇṭs avarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭa pustakattiṉ 53- vatu pakkattil colliyirukkiṟatāvatu :- caṅkīta ratṉākaram cura attiyāyam 3-vatu pirakaraṇam 12-vatu culōkamutal 16-vatu culōkam variyil.
 “22 tantikaḷ pōṭṭa iraṇṭu vīṇi tayār ceytu koḷ. piṉ varukiṟapaṭi atic curuticēr. mutal tanti kūṭiyavari tāṭinanta cuttamāyirukkumpaṭi curuti cērttukkoḷ. aṭuttatu ataṟkuk koñcam kūṭutalāṉa curamāyirukkaṭṭum. ippaṭiyē maṟṟa 22 tantikaḷiyum cērttukkoḷ. itil enta iraṇṭu tantikku naṭumattiyilum 3-vatu curam uṇṭākātapaṭi yirukkaṭṭum. ippaṭi oṉṟiṉpiṉ oṉṟāṉa curaṅkaḷ curutiyeṉṟaḻikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ippaṭic cērttukkoṇṭāl 4 curutikaḷiyuṭiya ṣaṭjama māṉatu 4-vatu tantiyil niṟkiṟatu. riṣapamāṉatu 5-vatu tantiyi liruntu 3 curutikaḷuṭaṉ amikiṟatu. 2 curutikaḷiyuṭiya kāntāram 8, 9-vatu tantikaḷil varum. mattimam 4 curutikaḷi yuṭiyatāy 10-vatu tanti mutal amikiṟatu. 4 curutikaḷi yuṭiya pañcamatti 14-vatu tanti mutal eṇṇip pōṭṭuk koḷ. 3 curutikaḷi yuṭiya tivatatti pañcamattiṟku mēlākappōṭu. 2 curutikaḷi yuṭiya niṣātatti tivatattiṟku mēl pōṭu ippaṭiyāṉāl 22-vatu iṭattil niṣātam varukiṟatu.”
mēlē kāṭṭiya iraṇṭuvitamāṉa moḻipeyarppum orē karuttuṭi yavikaḷāyirukkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟu nāṉ nampukiṟēṉ. āṉāl iti vevvēṟuvitamāka arttam paṇṇikkoḷḷukiṟatiṉāl curuti kaḷuṭiya niccayamum oṉṟāyirāmal palavāyirukkiṟatu. itōṭu caṅkīta pārijātak kārar eḻutiya muṟippaṭi ceyyum curutikaḷiyum mēṟṟici caṅkītattiṉ curutikaḷiyum karnāṭaka caṅkītattiṉ curutikaḷiyum kalantu tuvāvimcati curutikaḷ eṉṟa colliyum viṭṭuviṭāmal, taṟkālattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷum keṭṭuppōkāmal irukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu niṉittu pūrvīkam navīṉam eṉṉum iru muṟikaḷiyum niraval ceytu, ippaṭi vaḻaṅka vēṇṭum eṉṟum ippaṭittāṉ nammuṭiya kāṉam irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟum anēka curuti muṟikaḷ veḷiyiṭappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. ivvev vēṟu vitamāṉa apippirāyaṅkaḷip pārkkiṟa evarukkum intiya caṅkītattiṉ paritāpamāṉa nili teriyāmal pōkātu. makā paricuttameṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṟa jalam eppaṭi intiyāvil kavaṉikkap paṭāmal pōkiṟatō, appaṭiyē intiya caṅkītamum nāḷaṭivil kavaṉikkappaṭāmal maliṉa maṭintatu. eṉṟālum pūrvamutal maṉaṉam ceytu maṉatil kāppāṟṟum mika uttamamāṉa muṟiyiṉāl aṅkaṅku cila amcaṅkaḷ pēṇappaṭṭu, pūrvatti ñāpakappaṭutta viḷaṅkik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷik koṇṭu ippaṭiyirukka vēṇṭu meṉṟu rucuppaṭuttuvatu ilakuvāṉa kāriyamā yilli. āyiṉum, intiya caṅkītattiṉ curutikaḷip paṟṟi nāṉum cila vārttikaḷ colla niṉikkiṟēṉ. ‘nāṉ curutikaḷip paṟṟic colvatu cariyāyirukkum, intiya caṅkītaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷiṉ vivakāram itōṭu muṭintuviṭu’ meṉṟu itaṉ muṉ eḻutiya kaṉavāṉkaḷ eṇṇiya paṭiyē nāṉum eṇṇiya eṇṇatti maṉṉikkak kēṭṭuk koḷḷukiṟēṉ. cilar mikunta ciramattuṭaṉ taṅkaḷ kālattiyum poruḷiyum celaviṭṭut taṅkaḷ apippirāyatti veḷiyiṭṭirukkum nūlkaḷāṉavi eṉ maṉatit tūṇṭiyatiṉimittamē nāṉum itippaṟṟic cila vārttikaḷ colla nēriṭṭatu. taṟkālattil, caṅkīta cāstiraṅkaḷil mikap paḻimi yāṉatum, anēka viṣayaṅkaḷ aṭaṅki yiruppatumāṉa caṅkīta ratṉākarattil curutikaḷip paṟṟic collum attiyāyatti, curutikaḷip paṟṟi vicārikkum yāvarum mikunta kavaṉattōṭu paṭittiruppārkaḷ. avikaḷil colliya curutikaḷ muṟṟilum vaḻakkattil illāmal oḻintupōyiṉaveṉpatu, curutikaḷip paṟṟi eḻutiya kaṉavāṉkaḷiṉ kaṇakkukaḷik koṇṭu tiṭṭamāyt terikiṟatu. ṣaṭjam, mēl ṣaṭjam eṉṟa iraṇṭu curaṅkaḷit tavira vēṟē entac curutiyāvatu caṅkīta ratṉākarattil colliyirukkum tuvāvimcati curuti kaṇakkukku ottirukkavilliyeṉṟu kāṇkiṟēṉ. vekukālamāy vaḻaṅki vanta muṟiyil cila cantēkaṅkaḷi nīkka niṉittu atiyē muṟṟilum māṟṟumpaṭiyāka niṉippatu niyāyamalla. mēlum curutikaḷippaṟṟi vevvēṟu vitamāy apippirāyam colluvataṟkuriya niyāyaṅkaḷ tiṭṭamāyc collappaṭāmiyiyum collappaṭṭavikaḷum oru oḻuṅki aṉucarikkappaṭavilli eṉpatiyum kāṇkiṟēṉ.
caṅkīta ratṉākararuṭiya cūttiraṅkaḷukkuc cariyāka arttam paṇṇap paṭavilli eṉṟu naṉṟāp pulappaṭukiṟatu. vaṭatēcattil vaḻaṅki varum kāṉattiṉ mattiyilirukkum kaṉavāṉkaḷ curutiyip paṟṟi eḻuti yirukkum vevvēṟu apippirāyaṅkaḷip pārttāl, taṟkālattil vaḻaṅki varum kāṉam allatu caṅkīta ratṉākararuṭiya apippirāyam eṉṟa iraṇṭil oṉṟu tavaṟutaluṭiyatāyirukka vēṇṭum. mika nuṭpamāṉa oru muṟiyi mikavum culapamāṉa vārttikaḷāl coṉṉārēyoḻiya kaṭiṉamāṉa vārttikaḷil avar collavilli. ellāriyuṅ kāṇakkūṭiyatum, ellāruṅ kāṇakkūṭiyatumāṉa taṉatu kaṇṇi, oruvaṉ tāṉē kāṇā tirukkiṟatu eppaṭiyō appaṭiyē ellārukkum ilakuvāka viḷaṅkakkūṭiya ivviṣayamumirukkiṟatu. ivviṣayattil cāraṅkar muṟippaṭi ceykiṟō meṉṟu cila amcaṅkaḷil ottu arttam paṇṇum cakastiraputti, rājā curēntira mōkaṉatākūr pōṉṟa mutal vakuppāriyum, intustāṉ kītamuṟippaṭi yeṉṟu arttam paṇṇum tēvāl, kiḷameṇṭs pōṉṟa iṭi vakuppāriyum, mēṟṟicic caṅkītattil vaḻaṅkum curaṅkaḷiyum, maṟṟum avāntara curaṅkaḷiyum kalantu itutāṉ karnāṭaka caṅkītam eṉṟu collum kaṭivakuppāriyum, avarkaḷ muṟikaḷiyum iṉṉavi yeṉṟu curukkamāka vicārittu ataṉpiṉ caṅkīta ratṉākararuṭiya cariyāṉa apippirāyam iṉṉateṉṟu terintukoṇṭu, karnāṭaka caṅkītattil vaḻaṅkivarum curutikaḷip pārppatu nallateṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu.








mutalāvatu.
I. cāraṅkar curuti muṟippaṭi aḷavil ottirukkum mutal vakuppu.
1. cakastiraputti avarkaḷ colliyirukkum 22 curutiyiṉ muṟi.
taṟkālattil pūṉā kāyaṉa camājattiṉ Honorary Secretary yum nam intiya caṅkītam mikunta viruttiyaṭiya vēṇṭumeṉṟu mikunta pirayāci eṭuttukkoṇṭavarumākiya cakastiraputti curutiyip paṟṟi eḻutiya apippirāyam piṉvarumāṟu.
Hindu Music and the Gayan Samaj. Part ii. P. 13.
“If a monochord with moveable bridge be taken, and a space equal to 44 units be measured and the Bridge shifted to this point, the string when struck will yield a note; if we start with this note as the tonic or key-note and run through the Gamut by shifting the bridge (the Sanskrit writers affirm) the following facts will be observed. Sa will be produced at the distance 44; Ri at 40; Ga at 37; Ma at 35; Pa at 31; Dha at 27; Ni at 24; and Sa agin at 22; but the latter Sa will be twice as intense as the former.”
“tanti oṉṟi eṭuttukkoṇṭu atil aṅkumiṅkum taḷḷi vikkakkūṭiya oru ciṟu marappālamum vittukkoḷvōm. ētāvatu oru nīḷatti eṭuttukkoṇṭu ati 44 caripaṅku kaḷākap pirittuk koṇṭu anta marappālatti ataṉ kaṭici yil vittut tantiyi mīṭṭiṉāl oru curam pēcukiṟatu eṉṟu vittuk koḷḷuvōm. intac curatti ṣaṭjamamāka vittuk koṇṭu, antap pālatti aṅkumiṅkum taḷḷuva tiṉāl oru stāyiyiluḷḷa curamellām terintupōkum eṉṟu kiranta nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. atu eppaṭi yeṉṟāl 44-vatu pirivil ṣaṭjamum 40-vatu pirivil riṣapamum, 37-vatil kāntāramum, 35-vatil mattimamum, 31-vatil pañcamamum, 27-vatil tivatamum, 24-vatil niṣātamum mēl stāyi ṣaṭjamam 22-vatilum pēcum. mēl stāyi ṣaṭjamam muntiṉa ṣaṭjamatti viṭa irupaṅku uḷḷatāyirukkum.”
ivar colliya kaṇakku ipputtakattip pārkkum yāvarukkum naṉṟāy viḷaṅkum paṭi mutal 22 curutikaḷukku ivar koṭukkum aḷaviyum ataṉpiṉ 32 aṅkulattil avikaḷiṉ aḷavukaḷiyum, ataṉ piṉ oru stāyiyil enta iṭattil avikaḷ niṟkiṟateṉṟu kāṭṭum piṉṉattiyum ātāra ṣaṭjam 540 viparēṣaṉāṉāl maṟṟa curaṅkaḷiṉ ōcikaḷiṉ alikaḷ ivvaḷaveṉpatiyum pirittukkāṭṭum aṭṭavaṇi itōṭu cērkkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
kavaṉippu - curutikaḷip paṟṟiya apippirāyam palapalavāyum avar kaḷuṭiya aḷavum, ōcikaḷiṉ alikaḷum vevvēṟāyumiruppatiṉāl, ottup pārppatu caṅkaṭam. ākiyāl ellōrum ilakuvāy aṟintu koḷḷak kūṭiyatāka tantiyiṉ nīḷam 32 aṅkulamākavum ōciyiṉ alikaḷ 540 āyum vittuk koṇṭu avaravarkaḷ kaṇakkukku māṟṟi yirukkiṟēṉ.
mēlum 22 curutikaḷ cērkkum muṟi coṉṉa cāraṅkatēvaruṭiya muṟikku itu muṟṟilum ovvātatāyiruntālum maṟṟavikaḷōṭu ottup pārppataṟku aṉukūlamāka ovvoru curutikkum ittaṉi ceṉṭskaḷ eṉṟum avikaḷ ovvoṉṟukkumuḷḷa pētam iṉṉateṉṟum aṟintu koḷḷakkūṭiya kaṇakkum itōṭu cērttirukkiṟēṉ. iti vācikkiṟavarkaḷ culapamāy ottuppārttukkoḷḷak kūṭumātalāl avi collāmal viṭappaṭṭaṉa.









1-vatu aṭṭavaṇi.
intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷ iṉṉavi yeṉṟu makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi cakastiraputti avarkaḷ apippirāyattik kāṭṭum tuvāvimcati curutiyiṉ aṭṭavaṇi

* ivvaṭiyāḷamuḷḷa kaṇakkukaḷ innūlukkuriyavi.

kuṟippu - ivar tantiyiṉ nīḷam 44 eṇkaḷ eṉṟu vittuk koṇṭu, atiṉ mattiya pākattil tārastāy ṣaṭjam pirikkiṟār. itu yāvarum ilakuvāy aṟintu koḷḷak kūṭiyatē. tāra ṣaṭjattiṟkuk kīḻ uḷḷa mattiyastāy 22 eṇkaḷ nīḷamuḷḷatākiṟatu. inta 22 eṇkaḷil ovvoru eṇṇukku ovvoru curutiyāka 22 curutiyākiṟatu. tiruṣṭāntamāka 44 eṇkaḷiṉ nīḷattiyum 44 aṅkulameṉṟu vittuk koḷvōmēyāṉāl ovvoru aṅkulattukkum ovvoru curutiyāka 22-vatu curutiyil mattiyastāyi amikiṟatu. ivar ṣaṭjattiṟkuriya 4 curutikaḷil mutal curutiyi ātāra ṣaṭjamamāka vittuk koḷḷukiṟār. 44-l iruntu 41 vari ṣaṭjamattiṟkuriya 4 curuti kaḷākavum 40, 39, 38 riṣapattiṟkuriya 3 curutikaḷākavum 37, 36 kāntārattuk kuriya 2 curutikaḷākavum 35, 34, 33, 32 mattimattiṟkuriya 4 curutikaḷākavum 31, 30, 29, 28 pañcamattiṟkuriya 4 curutikaḷāka vum 27, 26, 25 tivatattiṟkuriya 3 curutikaḷākavum 24, 23 niṣātattiṟkuriya 2 curuti kaḷākavum varukiṟatu. ākavē, ṣaṭjamam 4, riṣapam 3, kāntāram 2, mattiyamam 4, pañcamam 4, tivatam 3, niṣātam 2 āka 22 curutikaḷ eṉṟu collukiṟār. itil nām kavaṉikkakkūṭiyatu oṉṟu uṇṭu. cāraṅka tēvar caṅkītaratṉā karattil uṉṉāl kuṟinta ōciyi mutal tantiyil vittukkoḷ. ataṟku mēl varakkūṭiya ōciyi iraṇṭām tantiyil vittukkoḷ. iraṇṭukkum naṭuvil vēṟu ōciyuṇṭākak kūṭātu. ippaṭip paṭip paṭiyāka ōcikaḷik kūṭṭik koṇṭu pōka avikaḷ tīviramākumeṉṟu ōciyik kuṟittuc coṉṉārē yoḻiyat tantikaḷiṉ aḷavil avikaḷ ottirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu collavilli yeṉṟu nām mukkiyamāyk kavaṉikka vēṇṭum. itaṟku arttam paṇṇiṉa cakastira putti eṉpavar caṅkīta ratṉākarar mantarastāyi ōci oru paṅkāṉāl mattiyastāy iru maṭaṅkum, tārastāy mattiya stāyiyiṉum iru maṭaṅkumāka irukkumeṉṟu colliyirukkiṟati aṟivār eṉṟu niṉikkiṟēṉ. appaṭiyāṉāl mattiya stāyikkuḷḷa eṇkaḷil atāvatu 22 aṅkulattil atu muṭivākiṟa tāṉāl tārastāy 11 aṅkulattōṭu muṭivaṭiya vēṇṭum. ippaṭiyē mēl pōkappōka, aḷaviṉ eṇkaḷ pāti pātiyākak kuṟintum ōciyiṉ alikaḷ iraṇṭu iraṇṭu maṭaṅkāka mēl kūṭiyum pōkiṟateṉṟu aṟiyalām. itip pōlavē oru stāyi yiluḷḷa curaṅkaḷum paṭippaṭiyākak kūṭutalāki iraṇṭil muṭivaṭikiṟatu. oru tantiyiṉ pātikkukkīḻāka mattiya stāyiyum pātikkumēl nālil oṉṟil tāra stāyiyum eṭṭil oṉṟil atitārastāyiyum jiyā meṭrikal purōkiraṣaṉpaṭi (Geometrical Progression) pēcukiṟati nām yāvarum aṟivōm. itupōlavē, oru stāyi yiluḷḷa ovvoru curamum paṭippaṭiyākat tantiyiṉ nīḷattil jiyā meṭṭirikal purōkiraṣaṉpaṭi kuṟintu koṇṭē pōkiṟatu. tāra ṣaṭjattiṉ mēluḷḷa riṣapam mattiya stāyiyiṉ riṣapattiṉ aḷavil pātiyākak kuṟinta teṉṟum mattiya stāyiyiṉ riṣapam tārastāyi yiṉ riṣapattiṉ tanti aḷaviṟku 2 maṭaṅku atikamāṉateṉṟum nām pārkkalām. āṉāl tārastāyiṣaṭjattiṉ kīḻuḷḷa niṣātam mēluḷḷa riṣapattiṉ aḷavukku cumār 9l oṉṟu kūṭiyirukkiṟatu. tantiyiṉ aḷavil riṣapam ippaṭik kuṟintiruntālum ōciyiṉ aḷavil riṣapam cumār 8l oṉṟu kūṭi yirukkiṟatākak kāṇpōm. ippaṭiyē capta curaṅkaḷum oru stāyiyil mēl pōkappōka ōciyil kūṭiyum tantiyil kuṟintumirukka vēṇṭi yatu. appaṭi illāmal camamākat tantiyi aḷaviṭum pōtu ōciyil vēṟupaṭṭirukkum. atāvatu caṅkīta ratṉākarar colliya ōciyiṉpaṭi atu ottirukkamāṭṭātu. muṭivāka ōciyiṉ aḷavil oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu pēta miṉṟi oṟṟumiyāy, orē aḷavāy mēlē pōka vēṇṭumeṉṟu coṉṉa cāraṅkatēvar apip pirāyattiṟku, tantiyiṉ aḷavil oṉṟaṟ koṉṟu pēta miṉṟi ottirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟa cakastiraputti avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyam muṟṟilum ōciyil ovvātu eṉṟu nāṉ niṉikkiṟēṉ. āṉāl nāta māṉatu cama aḷavāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟa apippirāyam ivaruk kuṇṭāṉati nām mikavum kavaṉikka vēṇṭum. ivar tantiyil eṭuttuk koṇṭa aḷaviyum, cāraṅkatēvar eṭuttukkoṇṭa aḷaviyum itiṉ piṉvarum aṭṭavaṇiyil kāṇalām. appaṭiyē ivar eṭuttuk koṇṭa kaṇakkiṉpaṭi varum ōciyiṉ aḷavukkaṇakkiyum cāraṅkatēvar eṭuttuk koṇṭa kaṇakkiṉpaṭi varum ōciyiṉ aḷavukkaṇakkiyum itiṉ piṉvarum aṭṭavaṇi yil pārkkalām.
ivar oru stāyiyi 22 pākaṅkaḷākap pirittu atil mutal nālu curutiyiyum ṣaṭjamattukkuriya teṉkiṟār. āṉāl ṣaṭjamattiṉ nālāvatu curuti mēruviṉiṭamākap pēcukiṟati nām yāvarum aṟivōm.






iraṇṭāvatu.
rājā curēntira mōkaṉa tākōr avarkaḷiṉ 22 curutiyiṉ muṟi.
ivar kalkattāvāci. nam intiya caṅkītattiṟku utaviyāyirukkum vāttiyaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟic cēkarittu pārīs nakarattiṉ kaṇkāṭcikku aṉuppi, intiya caṅkītam mēṟṟici yārukkum viḷaṅkum vaṇṇam anēka nūlkaḷ ceytavar. 1875-m varuṣattil nam intiya cakkaravarttiṉi avarkaḷ pērālum avarkaḷ muṉṉōrkaḷ pērālum “vikṭōriya kītikā” eṉṟa caṅkīta pustakam eḻutiṉavar. intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷik kāṇpataṟkāka aṭiyil varum vivarappaṭi pirittirukkiṟār. atippaṟṟi ivar eḻutiya pustakam eṉakkuk kiṭikkāviṭṭālum tēvāl eḻutiyirukkiṟatik koṇṭu ivaratu curuti muṟiyic collukiṟēṉ.
Hindu Musical Scale and 22 Srutis by K.B. Deval P. 34.
Mr. Deval says that he (Raja Surendra Mohan Tagore) divided the whole speaking length of the wire into two halves, the whole length giviing the sa or fundamental note and the half giving this sa the octave; both these notes are correct. Agin he divided the first half into two equal parts each being 1/4 of the whole length. The first quarter of the wire he subdivided into 9 equal parts calling each part a Sruti. And at the end of the 9th part is sounded a note ma (at 1/4 of the wire) which is correct. In the next quarter of the wire he made 13 equal subdivisions each being also called a Sruti. Thus in all he got the 22 Srutis.
 “atāvatu oru tantiyi iraṇṭu camapākamākap pirikkiṟār. muḻut tantiyil ātāra ṣaṭjam pēcukiṟatu. ataṉ pātiyil tāra ṣaṭjam pēcum. inta nōṭṭukaḷ cariyāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. mutal pātiyi iraṇṭu camapākamākap pirikkiṟār. atil ov voṉṟum mottattil nālil oṉṟākiṟatu. mutalāvatu 1/4 paṅku tantiyi 9 camapākaṅkaḷākap pirikkiṟār. atil ov voṉṟum oru curutiyeṉṟu collukiṟār. atil oṉpatāvatu pākattil muṭikiṟa curuti ma veṉṟu aḻikkap paṭum. atuvum cariyāyirukkiṟatu. tantiyiṉ aṭutta kāl pākatti 13 cama pākaṅkaḷākap pirikkiṟār. itaṉ ovvoru pākamum ovvoru curuti eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu. āka mottattil 22 curutikaḷum kiṭittirukkiṉṟaṉa.”
ṣaṭjamum, mattimamum cariyāyirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu tēvāl tam muṭiya pustakam 34, 35-m pakkaṅkaḷil collukiṟār. itik kavaṉikki yil oru stāyiyi 22 curutikaḷākap pirikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu avar apip pirāyappaṭukiṟār eṉpatu tiṭṭamāyt terikiṟatu.
āṉāl cakastiraputtiyavarkaḷuṭiya apippirāyattiṟkum, ivarkaḷu ṭiya apippirāyattiṟkum koñcam vittiyācamiruppatāka nām aṟiyalām. atāvatu, tantiyiṉ nālil oṉṟi 9 pākamākavum atiṉ mēluḷḷa nālil oṉṟi 13 camapākaṅkaḷākavum vakuttatē. mottattil camapākaṅkaḷeṉṟu niṉittatu cariyāyiruntālum, curastāṉaṅkaḷil caṟṟup pētamiruppatāka nām pārkkalām. oru stāyiyi 22 camapākaṅkaḷākap pirikkiyil caka stira puttiyiṉ apippirāyattiṉpaṭi 12-vatu curuti anta stāyikku matti yākiṟatu. appōtu 10-vatu curutiyākiya mattimattiṟkumēl 2 curutikaḷ cērntu varum. atāvatu mattimattiṉuṭiya 4 curutikaḷil 3vatu curutimatti yākum. āṉāl rājā curēntiramōkaṉa tākōr apippirāyattilō oru stāyiyiṉ mattiyapākamāṉatu 9 curutikaḷuṭiya mattimam ākumeṉṟu collukiṟār. cakastiraputtiyavarkaḷ mattiyastāyiyi caripākaṅkaḷākap pirikkiyil 12-vatu iṭam ippōtu nām cātāraṇamāyc collik koḷḷukiṟa mattimamākiṟatu. atāvatu 3/4 ākiṟatu. ivar kaṇakkiṉpaṭi atu mūṉṟāvatu mattimam ākum. āṉāl ivar 10-vatākac collum mutal mattimamāṉatu tantiyiṉ caripātiyil varukiṟatu. iraṇṭu curutikaḷ cakastira putti collum aṭṭavaṇikkuk kuṟintu varukiṟatākat terikiṟatu. matti mattiṉ nālu curutikaḷil mutalāvatu curuti tantiyiṉ pātiyil varukiṟa teṉṟu ivarum 3-vatu curuti tantiyiṉ caripātiyil varukiṟateṉṟu cakastira puttiyum collukiṟati nām kavaṉikka vēṇṭum.
tantiyiṉ nūlil oru pākattil mattimam caṟṟu muṉpiṉ varukiṟatā yiruntālum ataṟkuk kīḻ camamāka 9 paṅkukaḷ paṇṇavēṇṭu meṉṟum ataṟkumēl camamāka 13 paṅkukaḷ paṇṇavēṇṭumeṉṟum avar colla villi. mēlum mantara mattiya tārastāyikaḷ tantiyiṉ aḷavil oṉṟu, ari, kālāka mēl pākap pōkak kuṟukip pōvatu pōl oru stāyi yikkuḷ varukiṟa curaṅkaḷum vara vēṇṭumēyoḻiya camapākaṅkaḷāka varavēṇṭiya niyāyamilli. eṉṟālum, ōcikaḷ camapākamāy varavēṇṭu meṉṟa apippirāyattukkup patil curutikaḷ cama aḷavāṉa tantiyil varavēṇṭumeṉṟu kuṟittatāṉatu nām kavaṉikka vēṇṭiyatu. ivar oru tantiyiṉ, nālil oru pākatti 9 camapākaṅkaḷākavum ataṟkumēl nālil oru pākatti 13 cama pākaṅkaḷākavum pirikkumpaṭic collukiṟār. appaṭip pirikkumpoḻutu 32 aṅkulat tantiyil iṉṉiṉṉa iṭaṅkaḷil curutikaḷ varukiṟateṉṟum ātāra ṣaṭjattiṉ ōciyiṉ alikaḷ 540-l iruntu kūṭutal ivvaḷavu varukiṟateṉṟum kāṭṭakkūṭiya aṭṭavaṇi etir pakkattil kāṇka.
inta aṭṭavaṇiyiyum cakastiraputti aṭṭavaṇiyiyum ottup pārp pōmēyāṉāl ātāra ṣaṭjattiyum tāraṣaṭjattiyum tavira vēṟu enta curaṅkaḷum ottirukka māṭṭātu. pañcamamum mattimamumē ottirukkā viṭṭāl nām itil collakkūṭiyatu eṉṉa irukkiṟatu? oru stāyiyil kiṭikkakkūṭiya inta iraṇṭu curaṅkaḷum mika mukkiyamāṉavi. caṅkītat til ivviraṇṭu curaṅkaḷumillātirukku māṉāl ṣaṭjamattiṟku vēṟu poruttamuḷḷa curam illāmaṟ pōkum. maṟṟu enta curaṅkaḷ vittiyācap paṭṭālum mattima pañcamaṅkaḷ oṟṟumi yāyirukka vēṇṭum. caṅkīta ratṉākār ippaṭic collavilli. mikat teḷivākac colli yirukkiṟār. āṉālum itiyē iṉṉum anēkar vevvēṟu vita arttam ceytiruk kiṟārkaḷ. avikaḷil cilavaṟṟi oṉṟiṉpiṉ oṉṟāyp pārppōm.
ivar oru stāyiyi 22 pākaṅkaḷākap pirittu atil mutal nālu curutiyiyum ṣaṭjamattukkuriyateṉkiṟār. āṉāl ṣaṭjamattiṉ nālāvatu curuti mēruviṉiṭamākap pēcukiṟati nām aṟivōm.
maṟṟavi yāvum iṅkē colvatu avaciyamalla. āṉāl, caṅkīta ratṉākarar ippaṭippaṭṭa paṅkuvītam collavilli.

2-vatu aṭṭavaṇi.
intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷ iṉṉavi yeṉṟu
rājā curēntira mōkaṉa tākōr avarkaḷ
apippirāyattik kāṭṭum tuvāvimcati curutiyiṉ aṭṭavaṇi.
caṅkīta ratṉākara muṟippaṭi.

* ivvaṭiyāḷamuḷḷa kaṇakkukaḷ innūlukkuriyavi

mūṉṟāvatu
II. cāraṅkar curuti muṟippaṭi intustāṉi kītam irukkiṟateṉṟu collum iraṇṭām vakuppu.
K.B. tēvāl avarkaḷiṉ 22 curutiyiṉ muṟi.
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi tēvāl (Mr. Deval) teṟku makārāṣṭira tēcattic cērnta caṅkili eṉṉumūriluḷḷavar. ivar intiya caṅkītattil oru stāyiyil 22 curutikaḷ irukkiṟateṉṟu oru nūl eḻutiyirukkiṟār. atil 22 curutikaḷ ceyyum vitattip paṟṟi eḻutiya vitikaḷ patiṉmūṉṟum piṉvarumāṟu :-
Sa1 (C1) Sa2 (C2)
1. “The whole length f the wire between the two fixed bridges gives the Fundamental Note. Sa1 (C1). Let the length of the wire be 36 inches, the note produced be called Sa1 (C1) and let its vibrations be 240 per second.
2. The note produced on half the length is in value equal to Sa1 (C1) and in pitch or vibrations it is double the Fundamental Note.
The note produced on the length 18” is therefore Sa1 (C1) itself, but one octave higher. Let this note be called Sa2 (C2) to distinguish it from Sa1 (C1) the F.N. ; then the vibrations of Sa2 (C2) are double i.e. 2 x 240 = 480 per second.
3. The pitch of a note or its vibrations are inversely proportional to the length of the wire.
This rule is a legitimate inference from the above two rules. Rule (1) permits us to take any length for the Fundamental Note (F.N.) and according to rule (2) if the length is halved the pitch is doubled, and if the length is doubled the pitch or the number of vibrations is halved. If therefore 1/3 length is taken the pitch or the number of vibrations produced will be trebled. Or by generalization :
4. The pitch varies inversely as the length and vice versa.
The above four rules may therefore be put in the form of a simple Formula for convenience of working and ready reference.
Let V0 = vibrations or pitch of the note on wire Ln inches long. Un = The vibrations or pitch of Sa1 (C1) the F.N. here = 240.
L = the length of the wire of the F.N. here = 36 inches = 36”
Then :
Vn x ln = U x l 	..... ..... 	(A)
	Vn = U x l/ln 	..... ..... 	(B)
	ln = U x l/Vn 	..... ..... 	(C)
therefore if Vn = 2 U
	ln = l/2	..... ..... 	(D)
Ma (F)
5. The note Ma (or the fourth note) is produced at the middle of the Fundamental Note and its octave.
The note Ma is therefore produced at half the length of Sa1 (C1 ) and Sa2 (C2 ) or at 1/2 (36” + 18”) = 1/2 (54”) = 27”. In other words the note of the wire 27 inches or 27” of the executive part of the wire will give out the 4th note or Ma (F) and by rule (4) formula (B) the pitch or vibrations of Ma (F) are equal to 320.
The formula (B) is :
Vn = U x l/ln; Here U = 240, l = 36 and ln = 27.
Vn = 240 x 36/27 = 320 = Vibrations of Ma.
And Formula (C) is :
ln = U x l/Vn  ~ ln = 240 x l/320 = 3/4 l = 3/4 x 36
or the length of Ma is 3/4 of the length of the F.N. and the Vibrations of Ma are 4/3 of the F.N. and it may be lid down :-
6. That the length of the wire of Ma (F) or the 4th note is 3/4 of that of the Fundamental Note and the Vibrations of Ma (F) are 4/3 of the Vibrations of the Fundamental Note Sa1 (C1).
Pa (G)
7. The fifth or Pa (G) note is produced on 1/3 or 2/3 of whole length of the wire. The former note is one octave higher than the latter.
The length of the wire is 36”. Therefore a length of 12” or 24” will give the fifth note Pa (G). But we want the length between 18” and 36” - the two limits of the octave. Therefore the length 24” is that which we require and it will give out the note Pa (G).
Let us apply the formule (B) and (C) to the case of Pa (G)
	Vn = U x l/ln 	..... ..... 	(B)
Substitute the values U = 240, l = 36 and ln = 24
	Vn = 240 x 36/24 = 360 = Vibrations of Pa (G) = U x 36/24 = 3/2 U
	or the vibrations of Pa (G) are 3/2 of its Sa1 or F.N.
and
	ln = U x l/Vn 	..... ..... 	(C)
	~ ln = 240/360 x l = 2/3 l = 24.
	or the length of Pa is 2/3 of its Sa1 (C1) or F.N.
	These facts may be noted down under the rule (8) below.
8. The length of the wire of Pa (G) or the fifth note is 2/3 of that of Sa1’s (C1 ) wire and its vibrations or pitch is 3/2 of that of Sa1 (C1 ).
Ri (D)
9. In the interval of a given octave Sa1 (C1 ) with Pa (G) and Ma (F) with Sa2 (C2 ) form perfect concords; it may be noted that Sa1 (C1 ) with Ma (F) and Pa (G) with Sa2 (C2 ), the inverted interval, form imperfect concords.
This rule is very important and is made use of in finding out the lengths and vibrations of the other notes Ri (D), Ga (E), etc., etc.
According to Rule (1) any length may be sid to give the fundamental note and its Pa will be the 5th note from it. This Pa will form a perfect concord with it. This gives us the following consonant notes.
F.N. Sa1 (C1) 	consonant note 	Pa or Ma (F)
Sa1 C1			”	Pa G
Ri D			”	Dha A
Ga E			”	Ni B
Ma F			”	Sa2 C2
Pa G			”	Ri2 D2
Sa2 C2			”	Pa2 G2 or Pa G.
Let us take Pa (G) itself as the starting or fundamental note; then its Pa or fifth will be D in the higher octave which may be called Ri2 (D2 ). Apply the formula (B)
Vn = U l/ln ; here U = 360, l = 24, and ln = 2/3 x 24; ~ ln = 16.
Rule 7 Substituting the values of U, l and ln
Vn = 360 x 24/16 = 540.
540 are the vibrations of Ri2 or Ri (D) in the 2nd octave. Therefore the vibrations of Ri in the first octave are = 1/2 x 540 = 270 Vide Rule (2).
Formula C is - ln = U x 1/Vn; substituting U = 240, l = 36 and Vn = 270.
We have ln = 240 x 36/270 = 32. Hence -
10. The length of Ri (D) is 32 inches and its vibrations are 270; or, the length is 8/9 l, and vibrations 9/8 U.
Dha (A)
11. The length of Ri is 32”. If we take this as the starting note then its Pa is Dha. Therefore the length of Dha is 2/3 of 32 = 211/3 by Rule 8 and its vibrations are 3/2 of 270 = 405.
Ga (E)
The length of Dha (A) is 211/3 and its vibrations are 405. Let us take Dha as the fundamental note (Rule 1); then Ga2 (E) or Ga in 2nd octave becomes its Pa or the 5th note (Rule 9). Therefore its length is 2/3 x 211/3 and vibrations 3/2 x 405; but these are for Ga2. Therefore according to Rule (2) the length of Ga is = 2 x 2/3 x 211/3 = 2 x 2/3 x 64/3 = 256/9 = 284/9
and the vibrations of Ga = 1/2 x 3/2 x 405 = 1215/4 = 3033/4.
Ni (B)
The length of Ga is 284/9 and its vibrations 3033/4 (Rule 12). If we take Ga as the Fundamental note, then Ni becomes its Pa or the fifth note. Therefore.
the length of Ni = 2/3 x 284/5 = 2/3 x 256/9 = 512/27 = 1826/27
and the vibrations of Ni = 3/2 x 3033/4 = 3/2 x 1215/4 = 3645/8 = 4555/8
Ga (E)
Agin
12. If the vibrations of Ga (E) be taken as 300 (and there is a reason for doing so) in place of
303 3/4 as obtined in Rule (2) above, then
ln = Ul/Vn  	(C); U = 240, l = 36 and Vn = 300.
~   ln = 240 x 36/300 = 144/5 = 284/5.
Hence the length of Ga = 284/5” and its pitch = 300.
The Ga (E) obtined by the foregoing process has 3033/4 vibrations and bears with the F.N. a complicated ratio viz 81 : 64. The Ga (E) obtined as the fifth harmonic when reduced by two octives has 300 vibrations and bears with the F.N. the simple ratio of 5 : 4; and it sounds more consonant with it. It is clearly heard on the bass string (the fourth, giving Sa or F.N.) of the Vina. Sanskrit writers have adopted this in preference to the other. They tested their notes by harmonics; the author of Ragavibodha clearly lays down this.
Ni (B)
13. If Ga (E) is taken as the Fundamental Note, then Ni (B) becomes its Pa the fifth in the same octave.
~ 300 x 3/2 = 450 = the vibrations of Ni by Rule (8);
and the length is 2/3 x 284/5 = 2/3 x 144/5 = 288/15 = 193/15 = 191/5;
i.e. The vibrations of Ni = 450
and the length of Ni = 191/5. Etc. Etc.
ca1, ca2
1. oru tanti pōṭṭa oru tampuruvil mēruvukkum meṭṭukkum naṭumattiyiluḷḷa tanti oṉṟeṉṟu vittuk koḷ. iti mīṭṭiṉāl ātāra ṣaṭjam pēcum.
Notes - antat tantiyiṉ nīḷam 36 aṅkula mirukkaṭṭum. itil pēcum curam ca1 (C1) eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭum. atiṉuṭiya viparēṣaṉ allatu ōciyiṉ alikaḷ 240 āyirukkaṭṭum.
2. antat tantiyiṉ caripātiyil ātāraṣaṭjam (C1) pōla oru ṣaṭjam pēcukiṟatu. ataṉuṭiya stāyiyil allatu ōci yiṉ aḷavil ātāra ṣaṭjat tiṟku 2 maṭaṅkāyirukkiṟatu. (C2)
itu tantiyiṉ 18 aṅkulattil pēcuvatiṉāl ca1 (C1) eṉṟu collappaṭum. āṉāl oru stāyi mēlāyirukkiṟatu.
iti ca2 (C2)  eṉṟu colluvōm.
itu 480 viparēṣaṉāyirukkum.
3. oru curattiṉ ōciyiṉ alikaḷ allatu viparēṣaṉ kiramamāyt tantiyiṉ aḷavukku māṟutalāka varukiṟatu.
inta viti itaṟku muntiṉa iraṇṭu vitikaḷiṉ niyāyatti aṉucarittuc collappaṭukiṟatu.
mutal viti tanti enta aḷavāyiruntālum atil ātāra ṣaṭjam pēcum eṉṟu collukiṟatu.
iraṇṭāvatu viti antat tantiyi iraṇṭu camapākamākap pirittāl ōciyāṉatu atil iru maṭaṅkākiṟateṉṟum antat tantiyi 2 paṅku nīḷam ceytāl atiṉ ōciyiṉ alikaḷ pātiyākiṟateṉṟum collukiṟatu.
ākiyiṉāl tantiyiṉ mūṉṟil oru pākatti eṭuttuk koṇṭāl anta viparēṣaṉ mūṉṟu maṭaṅku atikamākum. allatu potuvāka :
4. ōciyāṉatu tantiyiṉ aḷavukku māṟutalāka muṟiyē māṟukiṟatu.
tantiyum appaṭiyē ōciyiṉ aḷavukku māṟutalāka varukiṟatu.
mēṟkaṇṭa nāluvitikaḷum iṉimēl ceyyappōkiṟa vēli kaḷukku aṉukūlamāyum appōtikkappōtu ottiṭṭup pārppataṟku ilakuvāyum irukkumpaṭip piramāṇamāka vittuk koḷvōm.
Vn = lnil pēcappaṭum viparēṣaṉ allatu ōciyāyirukkaṭṭum. Un =  ātāra ṣaṭjattiṉ allatu C1 iṉ 240 viparēṣaṉukkuc cari.
L = ātāra ṣaṭjam piṟakkum tantiyiṉ nīḷam = 36 aṅkulam iṅkē vittuk koṇṭōm.
ākiyāl –
Vn x ln = U x l 	..... ..... 	(A)
Vn = U x l/ln 	..... ..... 	(B)
ln = U x l/Vn 	..... ..... 	(C)
ākiyāl Vn = 2 U camāṉamāṉāl
ln = 1/2	..... ..... 	(D)
ma
5. nālāvatu curamākiya mattimam (F)  ātāra ṣaṭjattukkum tāra ṣaṭjattukkum naṭumattiyil uṇṭākum.
atāvatu meṭṭiliruntu 27 aṅkulattil pēcukiṟatu.
muṉ coṉṉa kaṇakkiṉpaṭi ataṉuṭiya ōciyiṉ alikaḷ 240x36/27 = 320.
6. nālāvatu curamākiya mattimam niṟkum tantiyiṉ nīḷam ātāra ṣaṭjam pēcum tantiyiṉ nīḷattiṟku 3/4 ākiṟatu. matti mattiṉuṭiya ōciyiṉ alikaḷ ātāra ṣaṭjamattiṉ viparēṣaṉukku 4/3 ākiṟatu.
pa
7. aintāvatu curamākiya pañcamam G.) tantiyiṉ motta nīḷattil 1/3 allatu 2/3 l pēcukiṟatu.
1/3 l pēcukiṟa pañcamam 2/3l pēcappaṭum pañcamattiṟku 2 maṭaṅku.
tantiyiṉ nīḷam 36 aṅkulam.
itil 12 aṅkulattilum 24 aṅkulattilum pañcamam pēcukiṟatu.
ippōtu tantiyiṉ motta nīḷattiṟkum atāvatu 36 aṅkulat tiṟkum atiṉ pātiyākiya 18 aṅkulattiṟkum naṭu mattiyil enta iṭam eṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikka vēṇṭum.
pañcamam 24 aṅkulattilum 12 aṅkulattilum irukkiṟatu eṉṟu colliyirukkiṟōm. 24 aṅkulattilirukkum pañcamam ippoḻutu namakku vēṇṭum.
muṉcoṉṉa kaṇakkiṉpaṭi Vn  = 240 x 36/24 = 360.
atāvatu - 240 x 3/2 = 360.
8. pañcamam niṟkum tantiyiṉ nīḷam ṣaṭjamam pēcum tantiyiṉ nīḷattiṟku 2/3, appaṭiyē ataṉuṭiya ōciyiṉ alikaḷ ṣaṭjamattiṉ ōciyiṉ aḷavukku 3/2 āy irukka vēṇṭum.
ri
9. ippōtu kiṭitta oru stāyiyil ṣaṭjamamum pañcamamum, mattimamum ṣaṭjamum pūraṇamāṉa oṟṟumi yuṭiyavikaḷ.
nām kavaṉikka vēṇṭiyatāvatu, ātāra ṣaṭjamamum mattimamum, pañcamamum ca2vum koñcam kuṟinta oṟṟumi yuṭivikaḷ. inta viti mikavum ri, ka mutaliya curaṅkaḷiṉ aḷaviyum, ōciyiṉ alikaḷiyum kaṇṭupiṭippataṟku mikavum upayōka muḷḷatu.
mutal vitiyiṉpaṭi enta nīḷamāṉa tantiyilum ātāra ṣaṭjam piṟakkum. atiliruntu aintāvatu curam pañcamamākum. inta pañcamam, ṣaṭjamattōṭu oṟṟumiyuṭiyatu. itu piṉṉāl varum curaṅkaḷik koṭukkiṟatu.
ca1 		pa
ri 		ta
ka 		ni
ma		ca2
pa 		ri2
ca2 		pa2
eppaṭiyeṉṟāl :- pañcamatti ātāra ṣaṭjamamāka vittuk koḷvōm. atiliruntu ataṉ 5vatu curammēl stāyiyil pañcamamākum. atu mēl stāyiyiṉ ri2 ākum. mēl colliya kaṇakkiṉpaṭi atiṉ ōciyiṉ aḷavu 540. tantiyiṉ aḷavu 16 aṅkulamākum. ippōtu riṣapattiṉ ōci yiṉ aḷavukaḷ 540. appaṭiyāṉāl kīḻ stāyiyiṉ riṣapattiṉ aḷavu atil pātiyākiya 270 āka irukka vēṇṭum. 240 viparēṣa ṉukku 36 aṅkula tantiyāṉāl, 270 viparēṣaṉukku
240 x 36/270 = 32 ākum. ākavē
10. riṣapam 32 aṅkulattil pēcukiṟatu. atiṉuṭiya viparēṣaṉ 270. allatu intat tantiyiṉuṭiya nīḷam ṣaṭjama tanti nīḷattiṉ 8/9 ākiṟatu. atiṉuṭiya ōciyiṉ aḷavu 9/8 ākiṟatu.
ta
11. riṣapattiṉ tantiyiṉ nīḷam 32 aṅkulam. iti ātāra ṣaṭjamamāka vittukkoṇṭāl itaṉ 5vatu curam pa vākiya tivatamākum. ākiyiṉāl tivatam tantiyiṉ nīḷam 32 aṅkulattil 2/3. atāvatu tivatam ṣaṭjama tantiyil 2/3 x 32 = 211/3 aṅkulattil varukiṟatu. atiṉuṭiya ōciyiṉ alikaḷ = 3/2 x 270 = 405.
ka
ākavē tivatam tantiyiṉ 211/3 aṅkulattil pēcukiṟatu. atiṉuṭiya ōciyiṉ alikaḷ 405. ippōtu tivatatti ātāra ṣaṭjamamāka vittukkoḷvōm. appaṭiyāṉāl iraṇṭāvatu stāyiyiluḷḷa ka2 tivatattiliruntu 5vatu curamākum. atiṉ aḷavu 2/3 x 211/3 = 2/3 x 64/3 = 128/9 = 142/9
ataṉ ōciyiṉ alikaḷ = 3/2 x 405 = 6071/2. itu mēl stāyiyiluḷḷa kāntārattiṟkāka. ākiyiṉāl 2vatu vitiyiṉpaṭi kāntārattiṉuṭiya nīḷam 2 x 142/9 = 284/9. itu ātāra ṣaṭjamattiṟku mēl varum kāntāram. itiṉ ōciyiṉ alikaḷ = 1/2 x 6071/2 = 3033/4.
ippōtu kāntārattiṟkut tantiyiṉ nīḷam = 281/9. atiṉuṭiya ōciyiṉ alikaḷ = 3033/4.
ni
kāntāratti ātāra ṣaṭjamamāka vittukkoḷḷuvōmē yāṉāl ataṉu ṭiya 5vatu curam ni yākum. ākiyiṉāl niṣātattiṉ nīḷam = 2/3 x 284/9 = 2/3 x 256/9 = 512/27 = 1826/27.
appaṭiyāṉāl niṣātattiṉ ōciyiṉ alikaḷ
= 3/2 x 3033/4 = 3/2 x 1215/4 = 3645/8 = 4555/8.
maṟupaṭiyum
ka
12. kāntārattiṉ ōciyiṉ alikaḷ 3033/4kkup patil 300 eṉṟu eṭuttuk koḷvōmōyāṉāl (appaṭi eṭuttukkoḷḷukiṟa taṟku niyāyamumirukkiṟatu) muṉ coṉṉa cūttirattiṉpaṭi atiṉuṭiya tantiyiṉ nīḷam 284/5 ākum. muṭivāka kāntāram niṟkum tantiyiṉ aḷavu 284/5. ataṉuṭiya ōciyiṉ alikaḷ 300.
itaṟku muṉ nam cūttirattiṉpaṭi kāntārattiṟku 3033/4 ōci yiṉ alikaḷ varukiṉṟaṉa. ṣaṭjamattiliruntu niṣātat tiṟku 81/64 āka varukiṟatu. kāntāram iraṇṭu stāyikkuḷ 5vatu taṭaviyil 300 ōciyiṉ alikaḷōṭum ṣaṭjamat tiṟku 5:4 pōla varukiṟatu. atiṉuṭiya cattam ṣaṭjamat tōṭu mikavum cērntu irukkiṟatu. vīṇiyiṉ 4vatu tantiyil itu rompat teḷivākak kēṭkiṟatu. camaskiruta vitvāṉkaḷ ataṟkup patil iti eṭuttuk koṇṭiruk kiṟārkaḷ. atāvatu 81:64kkup patilāka 5:4 eṭuttirukkiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ itit taṅkaḷ ottu varum muṟiyōṭu (Harmonic) ottirukkiṟateṉṟu collu kiṟārkaḷ.
ni
13. kāntāratti nām ātāra ṣaṭjamamāka vittuk koṇṭōmāṉāl niṣātam atiṉuṭiya 5vatu curamākiṟatu. ākiyiṉāl 300 x 3/2 = 450 = niṣātattiṉuṭiya ōciyiṉ alikaḷ.
ataṉ nīḷam 2/3 x 284/5 = 2/3 x 144/5 = 288/15 = 193/15 = 191/5
kuṟippu :- makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi tēvāl avarkaḷ tuvāvimcati curutiyi aṟivataṟkāka vekunāḷ mikavum ciramappaṭṭirukkiṟāreṉṟu terikiṟatu. intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷ iṉṉavi yeṉṟu caṅkītattiyē toḻilākak koṇṭa vittuvāṉkaḷē vicārikkā tirukkiyil, tām vicārittu aṟintu, aṟintavaṟṟit tam tēcattār aṟiyap pustaka mūlamākap piracurappaṭuttiyataṟkāka nāṉ mikavum cantōṣappaṭukiṟēṉ. taṟkālattil vaḻaṅki varum caṅkīta cāstiraṅkaḷil irākavipōtamum ataṉpiṉ caṅkīta pārijātamum paḻamiyāṉa nūlkaḷtāṉ. āṉāl tuvāvimcati curutiyip paṟṟic collum muṟippaṭi ivar ceyyāmal irāka vipōta muṟippaṭic ceytirukkiṟār. pārijātakkārariṉ ṣaṭjama pañcama muṟippaṭi ivar curaṅkaḷip paṭippaṭiyāyk kaṇṭupiṭittuk koṇṭu pōvatil 5-vatu paṭiyil kaviliruntu ni kaṇṭupiṭikkum iṭattil kavukku 3033/4 vipa rēṣaṉil 300 āka eṭuttuk koḷḷac collukiṟār. itaṟku niyāya mirukkiṟateṉṟum collukiṟār. maṟṟavar kātukku iṉimiyāyirukkiṟa teṉṟatit tavira vēṟu niyāyam etuvum collavilli.
ṣaṭjama pañcama muṟippaṭi 4vatu aṭukkil atāvatu (1) ca pa (2) pa ri (3) ri ta (4) ta ka vil 33/4 viparēṣaṉ pētappaṭumāṉāl maṟṟa ovvoru aṭukkilum koñcam koñcamākak kūṭiyē vantirukka vēṇṭum. itu tavira maṟṟavarkaḷ 4/5 eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṟa Major Third kku ottirukka vēṇṭumē yeṉṟu niṉittu 3033/4 il 33/4aik kuṟittu 240kkum 300kkum eppaṭiyō appaṭi 4:5 irukkiṟateṉṟu collukiṟār. atu tavira 3033/4 ai kāntārat tiṟku mēluḷḷa oru curutiyākac collukiṟār. 240kku 3033/4 eppaṭiyō appaṭi 64kku 81 irukkiṟateṉṟu kaṇakkuk kuṟikkiṟār. 64kku 81 eppaṭiyō eṉṟa kaṇakki vittu mēlē pōvataṟkuk kaṭiṉamākum eṉṟu kuṟittuk koṇṭārō allatu mēṟṟici caṅkītattil kāṇappaṭum Major Third kkuc cariyāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu kuṟittuk koṇṭārō teriyavilli. āṉāl camaskiruta vittuvāṉkaḷ kātukku iṉimiyāyirukkiṟateṉṟu colluvatākak kāraṇam collukiṟār. appaṭiyē niṣātam 4555/8 vara vēṇṭiyatāka aḷantu kāṭṭiṉavar 450 āka varavēṇṭumeṉṟum tiruttik koṇṭār. 450kkup piṟaku 4555/8 oru curutiyāka vara vēṇṭumeṉṟu collukiṟār. ippaṭi oru curuti 4vatu 5vatu paṭikaḷil pētappaṭṭu varumpaṭi yāṉa kaṇakkuc cariyāṉa kaṇakkāka irukkumōveṉṟu cantēkikkiṟēṉ. maṟṟum nuṭpamāṉa kaṇakki itaṉ piṉ varum aṭṭavaṇiyil viparamāyk kāṇalām.

3-vatu aṭṭavaṇi.
intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷ iṉṉavi yeṉṟu makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi mu.kṣa.tēvāl avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyattik kāṭṭum tuvāvimcati curutiyiṉ aṭṭavaṇi.  caṅkīta ratṉākaram, irākavipōtam, pārijātam muṟippaṭi
* ivvaṭiyāḷamuḷḷa kaṇakkukaḷ innūlukkuriyavi
3-vatu aṭṭavaṇiyil 11-vatu kalattil 8-vatu variyil 300 ōciyiṉ alikaḷ varukiṟatākavum ataṟku aṭutta 9-vatu variyil 303.75 varukiṟa tākavum nām kāṇpōm. ṣaṭjama pañcama muṟiyāl 4-vatu aṭukkil namakkuk kiṭikka vēṇṭiyatu 303.75 ōciyiṉ alikaḷi yuṭiya kāntāramām. atil 33/4 kuṟittu 300 āka eṭuttuk koṇṭār. appaṭi eṭuttuk koṇṭālum atu mēṟṟiciyār muṟikkuc cariyā yirukkiṟateṉṟu coṉṉāl paravāyilli. cāraṅkar muṟippaṭi 22 curutikku itu muṟṟilum poruttamuṭiyatalla. itupōlavē 303.75 iruntu ṣaṭjama pañcamamāyp pōkumpōtu 455.625 vara vēṇṭiyatu. itaṟkup patil cumār aintariyēyarikkāl ōciyiṉ alikaḷit taḷḷi 450 eṉṟu 20-vatu variyil pōṭṭirukkiṟār. ituvum mēṟṟiciyār 8/15 eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum niṣātam. immuṟiyil 8/15 eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum mēṟṟiciyāriṉ muṟiyum ṣaṭjama pañcama muṟiyeṉṟu vaḻaṅkum pārijātakkārar muṟiyum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu teḷivākat terikiṟatu. ivviru muṟiyilum kiṭikkakkūṭiya curaṅkaḷ 22 eṉṟum itu cāraṅkar muṟiyeṉṟum colluvatu poruntumō? ippaṭippaṭṭa kaṇakku cāraṅkar collavē yilli. intiya caṅkītattukku itu poruttamākavumilli. ivar koṭuttirukkiṟa aṭṭavaṇiyiṉpaṭi 20 ceṉṭsil 1 curutiyum, 22 ceṉṭsil 6 curutikaḷum, 27 ceṉṭsil 3 curutikaḷum, 63 ceṉṭsil 1 curutiyum, 71 ceṉṭsil 4 curutikaḷum, 84 ceṉṭsil 2 curutikaḷum 90 ceṉṭsil 3 curutikaḷum, 92 ceṉṭsil 2 curutikaḷumāka 22 curutikaḷeṉṟu collukiṟār. cāraṅkatēvarō oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu paṭippaṭiyāy uyarntum oṉṟiṟkum maṟṟoṉṟukku muḷḷa iṭiveḷiyil vēṟu curam varāmalum curuti ceyyac collukiṟār.
ṣaṭjakirāmam, cāntārakirāmam, mattimakirāmameṉṟu cāraṅkar collum muṟippaṭi kirakacuram pāṭuvataṟku oṟṟumiyāṉa aḷavuṭiya vallaveṉṟu terikiṟatu. eṭṭāvatu kalattil ātāra ṣaṭjam 0 āṉāl 1, 2, 3, 4 riṣapaṅkaḷ muṟiyē 84, 112, 182, 204 eṉṟa ceṉṭskaḷāka varukiṉṟaṉa. itil muṟiyē 84, 27, 71, 22 eṉṟa ceṉṭskaḷ varukiṉṟaṉa. ivi oḻuṅkīṉamāṉa aḷavuṭiyaviyeṉṟum kiraka curam pāṭuvataṟku ēṟṟa aḷavilli eṉṟum teḷivākak kāṇpōm. mēlum ṣaṭjamam 4, riṣapam 3, kāntāram 2, mattimam 4, pañcamam 4, tivatam 3, niṣātam 2 eṉṟu ovvoru curattiṟkum ittaṉi curutikaḷ vara vēṇṭumeṉṟu colli mottam 22 eṉṟu coṉṉārē yoḻiya ri, ka, ta, ni eṉṟa curaṅkaḷukku navvālu curutiyirukkiṟatākac collavilli. ākiyiṉāl itu cāraṅkaruṭiya karuttallaveṉṟu teḷivākat terikiṟatu. maṟṟum ovvoru curutiyip paṟṟiyum iṅku colla avaciyamilli. aṭṭavaṇiyil teḷivākak kaṇṭu koḷḷalām.
tantiyip paṅku vittuk koṇṭu pōvatu atāvatu 1/2, 1/3, 1/4, 1/5, 1/6 eṉṟu vittuk koṇṭu pōvatu cariyāṉa aḷavil curaṅkaḷit tarātu. itu curutiñāṉam aṟṟavarkaḷukkē caṟṟu ēṟattāḻac cērttuk koḷḷum vitiyāka ēṟpaṭṭatu. iti Just Temperament eṉṟu colvatu tavaṟutalā yirukkalāmeṉṟu eṇṇukiṟēṉ. 4-vatu paṭiyil 33/4 pētamirukku māṉāl ovvoru paṭiyilum koñcam koñcam pētamirukkum eṉpatu niccayam. niccayamāṉa allatu nuṭpamāṉa oru vaḻi kaṇṭupiṭittiruntā rāṉāl mikavum naṉmiyāyirukkum. maṟṟum curutikaḷiyum curaṅkaḷiyum ivar kuṟikkum muṟiyāṉatu caṅkīta ratṉākarattiṉpaṭi cariyāṉa teṉṟu nāṉ niṉikkavilli. ākiyāl, atippaṟṟi nāṉ atikamākac colla villi. muṭivāka ivar caṅkīta ratṉākarattil colliya 22 curutikaḷic collāmal pārijātakkārar curaṅkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭikkum oru aḷavic colli capta curaṅkaḷiyum kuṟittuviṭṭu ataṟkup piṉvarum curaṅkaḷi yum curutikaḷiyum tam maṉampōṉa pōkkil kuṟikkiṟār eṉṟu tōṉṟu kiṟatu. ataṟku enta nūlilum ātāramilli. mēlum kāntārattiṟkum niṣātattiṟkum mēlvarum ciṟu curutikaḷiṉ iṭiveḷikaḷ pōṉṟa atāvatu 300- 3033/4, 450-4555/8 pōṉṟa ettaṉiyō iṭiveḷikaḷ varalām.
itu tavira capta curaṅkaḷiṉ varicikkiramattil iṭiveḷikaḷil ari curaṅkaḷiṉ varicikaḷiyum, curuti varicikaḷiyum kuṟippataṟkāka ivar vaḻaṅki varum muṟi camaskiruta nūlkaḷil collappaṭavilli. tāmāka vittuk koṇṭatākat tōṉṟukiṟatu. ṣaṭjamattiṟkum riṣapattiṟkum naṭuviluḷḷa iṭiveḷiyi atāvatu 36 aṅkulamutal 32 aṅkula muḷḷa iṭiveḷiyi 4 curutistāṉaṅkaḷākap pirikkiṟār. atil mutalāvatu 1 pōlavum, (2) 21l 20 pōlavum (3) 16l 15 pōlavum (4) 10l 9 pōlavum (5) 9l 8 pōlavum muṟiyē 36 aṅkulattilum, 342/7 aṅkulat tilum, 333/4 aṅkulattilum, 322/5 aṅkulattilum, 32 aṅkulattilum pākikkiṟār. ivikaḷ oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu ēṟṟattāṭi nacciyāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivi muṟiyē ātāra ṣaṭjam 240iliruntu 252, 256, 2662/3, 270 āka varukiṉṟaṉa. mutal curuti ātāra ṣaṭjattukku 12 ōciyiṉ alikaḷ kūṭiyatākavum, iraṇṭāvatu muntiṉataṟku 4 viparēṣaṉ kūṭiṉatākavum, mūṉṟāvatu iraṇṭāvataṟku 102/3 kūṭiṉatākavum, nāṉkāvatu mūṉṟāvataṟku 31/3 kūṭiṉatākavum varukiṟatu. ippaṭiyē cila curastāṉaṅkaḷukku oṉṟaṟ koṉṟu ottirāta pala piṉṉaṅkaḷi upayōkikkiṟār. itu ivaruṭiya conta apippirāyam eṉṟu niṉikkiṟēṉ. 22 curutikaḷit teḷivāyk kāṭṭa vanta ivar eṅkēyāvatu oru iṭattil tavaṟippōṉāl, tiruttic colvatu pirayōcaṉamāyirukkum. āyiṉum ivar curutikaḷi aṟiya virumpiya viruppatti nāṉ mikavum meccikkoḷḷukiṟēṉ.
ivar ēṟpaṭuttiyirukkum curuti stāṉaṅkaḷip paṟṟik kiḷameṇṭs eḻutiya apippirāyam aṭiyil varumāṟu :-
In his Introduction to Hindu Musical scale and the 22 Srutis of Deval Mr. E. Clements says
“Secondly it will be found that although Mr. Deval did not test his Shrutees throughout by the harmonic intervals 5:4, 6:5 and 7:6 which may be called the Major Third, Minor Third and Septimal Third, they are clearly built up from those intervals.
“Indeed the importance attached to the Septimal intervals, that is those derived from the seventh harmonic, places the Music of India in the first rank of intellectual developments of the musical art. Some writers on Harmony have elaborated theories based upon the supposition that the subdominant (corresponding with Komal madhyam) and dominant seventh (that is Atikomal madhyam) are for all practical purposes the same note. No one who has attentively listened to Indian Ragas could entertin such an idea for a moment, as the Septimal Seventh, is very much flatter than the ordinary seventh, the interval 7:6 being easily distinguishable even by the untrined ear from the interval 6:5.
“The 22 shrutees are as Mr. Deval points out a selection from the total number of shrutees used in Indian Ragas and Raginis. Mr. Deval had not only to ascertin what shrutees were made use of by different singers but also to pick out the 22 which might justly be considered essential.”
“Mr. Krishnaji Mahadev Gokhale of Miraj who makes use of considerably more than 22 Shrutees has kindly sung over to me 83 Ragas and Raginis and given me the names of the notes used in them. It appeared to me that the extra shrutees used were chiefly those necessary to give the exact septimal intervals in some of those cases which I have marked ‘nearly’ in the footnote. An additional ‘Atikomal Gandhar’ of 280 relative number of vibrations was also used but in one Raga only. Mr. Deval tells me that some singers use a similar septimal ‘Atikomal Nishad’. The ‘Atikomal Nishad’ chosen by him is however a vital necessity as the fourth above madhyam and if the Shrutees are to be restricted to 22 I am afrid one must give up these two septimal notes, pleasing though their effect must be acknowledged to be.
“The intervals between each shrutee and next as given in Mr. Deval’s table D. are of no great significance.”
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi tēvāl avarkaḷiṉ 22 curutikaḷip paṟṟik kiḷameṇṭs avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyam.
“iraṇṭāvatāka, tēvāl eṉpavar tammuṭiya curutikaḷ ārmāṉik iṭiveḷikaḷākiya 5:4, 6:5, 7:6 muṟiyē atāvatu Major Third, Minor Third and Septimal Third eṉpavikaḷukku ottirukkiṟatō eṉṟu cōtittup pārkkāviṭṭālum, anta iṭiveḷikaḷi ātāramāy vittukkoṇṭu tāṉ curuti niccayam ceytirukkiṟār eṉpataṟkut taṭiyilli.
 “ēḻāvatu ārmāṉik curamākiya inta Septimal iṭiveḷiyi vicēṣittuk koḷvatē intiya caṅkītam. yuktipūrvamāy muṉṉukku vantirukkiṟa vittikaḷil mutaṉmiyāṉatu eṉpataṟku ōr aṟikuṟiyāyirukkiṟatu. kōmaḷamattimattiṟkuc cariyāṉa Subdominant  (atāvatu ārmōṉiyattil 6vatu curam) um atikōmaḷa mattimattiṟkuccariyāṉa ēḻāvatu Dominant um anēkamāy orē curantāṉ eṉṟa niccayattiṉ pēril cilar pala koḷkikaḷi stāpittirukkiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl intiya irākaṅkaḷi nuṭpamāyk kavaṉitta evarāvatu atu cariyeṉṟu oppukkoḷḷa māṭṭārkaḷ. ēṉeṉṟāl cātāraṇa ēḻāvatu curattiviṭa Septimal  curamāṉatu curuti kuṟintatu eṉṟum 7:6 eṉṉum iṭiveḷiyāṉatu 6:5 eṉṉum iṭiveḷiyiviṭa curutiyil kuṟintatu eṉṟum caṅkīta ñāṉamillātavarkaḷ kūṭa eḷitil kaṇṭukoḷḷalām.
“makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi tēvāl collukiṟapaṭi 22 curutikaḷum intiya irākaṅkaḷ irākiṉikaḷ ivikaḷil upayōkikkap paṭukiṟa curutikaḷiṉiṉṟu porunti eṭukkappaṭṭavi. avar pala vittuvāṉkaḷuṭiya caṅkītaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum iṉṉiṉṉa curutikaḷ avikaḷil upayōkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu mutalil niccayittup piṉpu vicēṣamāṉavi eṉṟu koḷḷattakka inta 22 curutikaḷi yum avikaḷiṉiṉṟu poruntiyeṭukka vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. 
“22 curutikaḷukku mēl anēka curutikaḷuṇṭu eṉṟu koṇṭa Miraj paṭṭaṇattu Krishni Mahadev Gokhale eṉpavar 83 irākaṅkaḷ irākiṉikaḷi eṉakkup pāṭikkāṭṭiṉatumallāmal avi kaḷil upayōkikkappaṭum curaṅkaḷiṉ pērkaḷum iṉṉiṉṉavi eṉṟu eṉakkuc coṉṉār. avar coṉṉa 22kku mēṟpaṭṭa curutikaḷ yāvum cariyāy Septimal iṭi veḷikaḷil varakkūṭiya curaṅkaḷ tāṉ. avikaḷil cilavaṟṟip pakkattiṉ aṭiyil “nearly”  (atāvatu koñcam kuṟiya cariyāṉatu) eṉṟa kuṟippāl kāṇpittirukkiṟēṉ. 280 tuṭikaḷuḷḷa iṉṉoru atikōmaḷa kāntāramum orē rākattil upayōkikkappaṭṭatākat terintatu. cila pāṭakarkaḷ atēvitamāy Septimal atikōmaḷa niṣātatti upayōkik kiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu collukiṟār. mattimattiṟku mēl nālāvatu curutiyāka varum inta atikōmaḷa niṣātam kaṭṭāyam irukka vēṇṭiyatu. atillāviṭṭāl jīvaṉilli. āṉāl curutikaḷ irupattiraṇṭu eṉṟu collum viṣayattil mikavum iṉimiyāṉa inta iraṇṭu cepṭimal curaṅkaḷiyum viṭṭuviṭa vēṇṭumē eṉṟu atika varuttappaṭukiṟēṉ. 
“makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi tēvāl pustakattil D. Table eṉṉum aṭṭa vaṇiyil inta curutikkum aṭutta curutikkum naṭuviluḷḷa iṭiveḷikaḷip paṟṟic colliyiruppatāṉatu atika mukkiya māṉatalla.”
Regarding Mr. Deval’s Shrutees Dr. Coomarasamy says :-
“It is true that Mr. Deval did not succeed in his endeavour to improve his case by importing aid and corroboration from scientific acoustics and Sanskrit philogy, but I think that certin of his crities fall into more serious error when they judge the results of his patient and invaluable experimental work by weakness or inaccuracies in his method of presentiton.”
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi tēvāl avarkaḷiṉ 22 curutikaḷip paṟṟi 
Dr. kumāracāmi avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyam.
 “Mr. tēvāl eṉpavar tammuṭiya koḷkikaḷi stāpippa taṟkāka nātattip paṟṟiya kōṭpāṭukaḷ aṭaṅkiya cāstira vitikaḷiyum samskiruta nūlkaḷiyum ātāramāka eṭutturittatu avarukku virōtamāy muṭintatēyoḻiya cātakamāy muṭiyavilli. āṉāl avaruṭiya koḷki yip paṟṟit taṅkaḷ apippirāyatti veḷiyiṭṭa anēkar, avar poṟumiyōṭum viṭāmuyaṟciyōṭum ceytu muṭitta arumiyāṉa nūlāṉatu avar koḷkikaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟa viṣayattil kāṇappaṭukiṟa kuṟṟaṅ kuṟikaḷ tappitaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟāl viyarttamāy viṭṭatu eṉṟu colvatāṉatu mikavum tappāṉa apippirāyam.”
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi tēvāl intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum tuvāvimcati curutikaḷic colla vantavar aṅkaṅku cila culōkaṅkaḷi eṭuttu mēṟkōḷākac collikkoṇṭu mēṟṟici caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷ collum eṉ ārmāṉik skēl (Enharmonic Scale) eṉṉum 18 curaṅkaḷi liruntu 13 curaṅkaḷi appaṭiyē eṭuttuk koṇṭu ataṟku mēlum kīḻumākac cila curutikaḷik kūṭṭi 22kku niraval ceytirukkiṟār. mēṟ kāṭṭiya 13 curaṅkaḷ varuvataṟkāka caṅkīta pārijātakkārar muṟiyic coṉṉār. maṟṟum 9 curutikaḷ varuvataṟkut takutiyāṉa niyāyam collavilli. intiyāvilē piṟantu vaḷarntu intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷic colla vantavar taṟkālattil vaḻaṅki varum kāṉaṅkaḷiluḷḷa irākam iṉṉa iṉṉa curutikaḷil varukiṟateṉṟu vistāramāy vicārittu aṉupava muṟippaṭic curutikaḷic collavu milli; allatu caṅkīta ratṉākarar curuti cērkkum muṟippaṭic curuti cērttu ati stāpikkavu milli; allatu caṅkīta pārijātakkārar colliya vaḻippaṭip pōy intiya caṅkīta curutikaḷ ippaṭittāṉ eṉṟu oru uṟuti kūṟavumilli. intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum nuṭpamāṉa curutikaḷ vāyiṉāl pāṭavum atu pōla vīṇiyil vācittuk kāṭṭavum kūṭiyatē yoḻiya curutikaḷuk kēṟṟa meṭṭu vittu vīṇiyil vācippatum ārmōṉiyam mutaliya vāttiyaṅkaḷil curam amippatum avikaḷil pāṭuvatum muṟṟilum kūṭāta kāriyam. appaṭic ceyyap pirayattaṉappaṭuvatu intiya caṅkītattiṉ vistārattik keṭuttu ataṉ aḻakum uṉṉatamumāṉa cikaraṅkaḷiyum uṭittup pāṭina paṭuttuvatākum. mēlum curutikaḷi aḷantu curam kaṇṭu piṭikkum pārijātakkāraruṭiya muṟiyip pōlotta orumuṟi yiyē mēṟṟiciyārum aṉucarittiruppatākat terikiṟatu. eṉṟālum avarkaḷ curaṅkaḷ oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu oṟṟumippaṭṭu iṉimi taruvataṟku aṉukūlamāka mēṟkaṇṭa aḷaviṉāl kiṭitta curaṅkaḷukkukkūṭṭiyum kuṟittum taṟkālam vaḻaṅkik koṇṭu varukiṟārkaḷ. eṉ ārmāṉik skēl (Enharmonic Scale) eṉṟu eṭuttuk koṇṭavar avikaḷil koñca māvatu māṟṟāmal eṭuttuk koṇṭālum oruvāṟu cariyāyirukkum. āṉāl ‘caṅkīta ratṉākararuṭiya apippirāyam itutāṉ, intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷ ivikaḷ tāṉ’ eṉṟu coṉṉatu caṟṟuk kavaṉikka vēṇṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu. eṉṟālum, intiya caṅkītattil taṉakkut terinta apippirāyaṅkaḷip piracurittu neṭunāḷ kavaṉikkāmal viṭṭuviṭṭa intiyarukku oru kiḷarcci uṇṭāvataṟkuk kāraṇamāyirunta ivaruṭiya muyaṟci mikavum pārāṭṭat takuntatāyirukkiṟatu.









nālāvatu.
ṭisṭirikṭ jaṭj E. kiḷameṇṭs avarkaḷiṉ curutiyiṉ muṟi.
kiḷameṇṭs (Mr. Clements) avarkaḷ intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷip paṟṟi iraṇṭu vitamāṉa kaṇakkukaḷik koṭukkiṟār. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu pātar, cāraṅka tēvarkaḷuṭiya apippirāyappaṭi 22 curuti kaḷip paṟṟiyatu. maṟṟoṉṟu K.B. tēvāl avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyattik kalantu tām cila cērttuc colluvatu. ivviraṇṭu apippirāyaṅ kaḷilum cila mukkiyamāṉa vacaṉaṅkaḷiyum kaṇakkukaḷiyum iṅkē eṭuttuc colli ataṉpiṉ cila kuṟippukaḷic colvatu mikavum avaciyamā yirukkiṟatu.
Introduction to the study of Indian Music, By E. Clements I.C.S. P.2.
“The present work deals with Hindustani music only; the author hopes to be able to show that a great part of it is directly traccable to the systems set forth in Bharatha’s Natya-Shastra of about the fifth century A.D., and the Sangit Ratnakar of the thirteenth century. These are the most closely reasoned and critically worded of the early text books. It is reputed that Sarangadev the author of the Sangit Ratnakar was an inhabitant of Kashmir. From internal evidence one would conclude that the music he describes is that if Hindustan. However the pandits of Southern India endeavour to appropriate him to themselves. The present writer hopes to show that it it only by doing violence to his theory that it can be applied to Karnatic music. Roughly speaking, Hindustani music may be sid to previl in the north and west of India and the eccan, while Karnatic music is confined to the south and cast. Many scales are common to both but the general spirit of the two systems is apparent from the scales which are first tught to beginners; in the west, the scale is the same as the just major scale of Europe in the south it is a chromatic scale (known in Hindustani music as the scale of the Raga Bhirava) with semitones between the first and second, third and fourth, fifth and sixth, seventh and eighth degrees.”
 “innūl intustāṉi caṅkītattippaṟṟi māttiram collap pōkiṟatu. itil collappōkiṟa anēka caṅkatikaḷ, ki.pi. aintāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭa parataruṭiya nāṭṭiya cāstiram eṉṉum nūlil collappaṭṭa muṟikaḷi liruntum, ki.pi. patiṉmūṉṟāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil uṇṭāṉa caṅkīta ratṉākaram eṉṉum nūliliruntum nērē eḻutap paṭṭavi eṉṟu innūlāciriyar tiruṣṭāntappaṭuttuvār. ivviraṇṭu nūlkaḷuntāṉ āti caṅkīta nūlkaḷil tarkka cāstira muṟikaḷuk kiṇaṅka eḻutappaṭṭavi. caṅkīta ratṉākara nūlāciriyarāṉa cāraṅka tēvar eṉpavar kāsmīra tēcattic cērntavar eṉṟu oppuk koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. uḷ nuḻintu anta nūli vācittāl avar pēcum caṅkītam intustāṉi caṅkītameṉṟu naṉṟāyt teriyum. āṉālum teṉṉintiya caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷ avarit taṅkaḷuk kuriyavareṉṟu koṇṭāṭukiṟārkaḷ. tammuṭiya koḷki intustāṉi caṅkītattukkuriyaviyēyallāmal karnāṭaka caṅkīttukkuriyaviyalla eṉṟu innūlāciriyar tiṭṭa māy rūpakāram paṇṇappōkiṟār. mēmpāṭāyc colla vēṇṭu māṉāl intustāṉi caṅkītamāṉatu intiyāviṉ vaṭakku, mēṟku, ṭakkāṉ (Deccan) pākaṅkaḷilum, karnāṭaka caṅkītam teṟkilum, kiḻakkilum upayōkikkappaṭṭu varukiṟatu eṉṟu collalām. iraṇṭu caṅkītattukkum anēka mūrccaṉikaḷ potuvāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl piratamattil ciṣyar kaḷukkuc collik koṭukkappaṭum mūrccaṉikaḷik kavaṉittāl iraṇṭiṉuṭiya amcaṅkaḷukkum uḷḷa vittiyācam teriyum. mēṟku tēcattil collik koṭukkappaṭum mūrccaṉi airōppiya mējar skēl (Major Scale) ai ottirukkiṟatu. teṟkē collik koṭukkappaṭuvatu ari curaṅkaḷākiya kurōmāṭik skēl (Chromatic Scale). itil muṟiyē mutalāvatu iraṇṭāvatu curattiṟkum, mūṉṟāvatu nāṉkāvatu curattiṟkum, aintāvatu āṟāvatu curattiṟkum ēḻāvatu eṭṭāvatu curattiṟkum naṭuvē ari curaṅkaḷ varum. iti intustāṉi caṅkītattil piravi rākam eṉpārkaḷ.”
mēlē kaṇṭa cila vacaṉaṅkaḷiṉāl tāṉ paratar cāraṅkatēvar kaḷuṭiya muṟiyi aṉucarittē 22 curutikaḷic collappōkiṟa tākavum ammuṟiyum intustāṉi caṅkītattiṟkē uriyateṉṟum teḷivākac collukiṟār. inta iṭattil cāraṅkatēvar hitarāpāt (Hyderabad) eṉṉum intustāṉi tēcattil oḷaraṅkapāt eṉṉum periya paṭṭaṇattukku arukiluḷḷa tulatapāt eṉṉum intustāṉi paṭṭaṇattil iruntataṉāl intustāṉikkuriya caṅkīta muṟikaḷi eḻutiyirukkiṟār eṉṟu kiḷameṇṭs niṉikka ētuvirukkiṟatu. eṉṟālum uṇmiyil avar nūli vicārippōmāṉāl anēka amcaṅkaḷil appaṭiyallaveṉṟu teriyavarum.
mutalāvatu, tuvāvimcati curutiyip paṟṟi eṭuttuk koḷvōm. tēvālum kiḷameṇṭsum collum apippirāyattiṟkuc caṅkīta ratṉākarariṉ apippirāyam muṟṟilum vēṟāyirukkiṟatu. kiḷameṇṭs collukiṟa apippirāyam intustāṉ caṅkītattiṟkēyuriyatu eṉṟu tiṭṭamāyc collukiṟapaṭi avviṭattil intustāṉi caṅkītattil pēr peṟṟa vittuvāṉ aptul karīm (Abdul Karim) mūlamāyt tām muka mukamāyk kēṭṭu oppuk koḷḷukiṟa curutikaḷāṉavi intustāṉi caṅkītattukkē yuriyatāyirukkalām. āṉāl cāraṅkatēvaruṭiya tuvā vimcati curuti muṟikku ottatāyirukka māṭṭāteṉṟu itaṉ piṉ varum cāraṅkatēvar tuvāvimcati curuti aṭṭavaṇiyil vivaramāyk kāṇalām.
iraṇṭāvatu, cāraṅkatēvar intutēcavāciyākiya cimmaṇarājaṉ camastāṉa vittuvāṉāyiruntatākavum avaruṭiya kēṭṭuk koḷḷutaliṉ pēril caṅkītaratṉākaram eḻutiṉatākavum terikiṟatu.
mūṉṟāvatu pirammā, viṣṇu, uruttiraṉ mutaliya mummūrtti kaḷiyum akāra, ukāra makāra, ōṅkāram eṉṉum pījāṭcāṅkaḷiyum, nālu vētaṅkaḷiyum, nālu jātikaḷiyum 7 curaṅkaḷukkum ati tēvatikaḷāṉa akkiṉi, pirammā, carasvati, araṉ, ari, vināyakaṉ, cūriyaṉ mutaliyavarkaḷiṉ pērkaḷiyum, avikaḷik kaṇṭupiṭitta intiraṉ, pirammā, cantiraṉ, viṣṇu, nāratar, tumpuru eṉṉum periyōrkaḷiṉ pērkaḷiyum, irākaṅkaḷiṉ pērkaḷiyum kavaṉikkiyil intustāṉi caṅkītattiṟkum caṅkīta ratṉā karattukkum evvitamāṉa campantamumilli yeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu.
nālāvatu makammatu kajiṉi paṭiyeṭuttu vanta kālattiyum (1024), ṭilliyil 1206l mutal mutal rājyam stāpitta makammatu kōriyiṉ kālattiyum, tēvakiriyil cōmarāja makārājaṉ kālattiyum nām ottiṭṭup pārttāl, cāraṅkatēvar intustāṉi caṅkītattiṟkāka inta nūl eḻutiyirukkamāṭṭāreṉpatu veḷiyākum. vintiya malikkut teṉpākattiṟku makamatiyar 1294-m varuṣattil vantatākat terikiṟatē yoḻiya ataṟku muṉ vantatākat teriyavilli. eppaṭiyiruntālum intustāṉ caṅkītattiṟkuriya curutikaḷi eḻutukiṟēṉ eṉṟu colvatil namakku evvitamāṉa āṣēpaṉiyumilli. āṉāl cāraṅka tēvar muṟippaṭi iti eḻutukiṟēṉ eṉṟu colluvatutāṉ muṟṟilum āṣēpaṉikku iṭimāyirukkiṟatu. kiḷameṇṭs tammuṭiya pustakam 6-vatu 7-vatu pakkaṅkaḷil piṉ varumāṟu collukiṟār.
Introduction to the study of Indian Music by E. Clements. P. 6, 7.
“He persevered for years at this investigiton deriving assistane from many of the best singers that India could produce. As regards most of the notes in use, his conclusions, when referred back to ancient theory, may be summed up in the statement that two Srutis make a just semitone, three Srutis a minor-tone, and four Srutis a major-tone. In respect of these notes the accuracy of his conclusions can fairly be sid to be beyond controversy.
*    *    *    *    *    *    *    *    *    *    *    *
“The author has through Mr. Deval’s courtesy, and with the help of Abdul Karim and other singers, been able to verify all the varidus scales mentioned in the following pages upon this instrument (Deval’s Sruti Harmonium).
“The following table describes the twenty-four notes in most frequent use, showing which of them are adopted in the Indian Harmonium, and their relationship with the ancient Srutis.”
“intiyāvil pūrṇa vittuvāṉkaḷ eṉṟeṇṇappaṭṭa mutal taramāṉa pāṭṭukkārariṉ utaviyik koṇṭu inta curuti ārāycciyil avar anēka varuṣaṅkaḷic celavaḻittār. ātiyil ēṟpaṭṭa vitikaḷiṉ muṟippaṭip pārttāl, upayōkattilirukkappaṭṭa curaṅkaḷip paṟṟi avar colva tāṉatu toki yāyp piṉ varumāṟākum. atāvatu, iraṇṭu curutikaḷ koṇṭatu oru jasṭ semiṭōṉ (Just semitone) mūṉṟu curutikaḷ koṇṭatu oru miṉar ṭōṉ (minor tone)  nāṉku curutikaḷ koṇṭatu oru mējar ṭōṉ (major tone)  eṉpatē. anta curaṅ kaḷip paṟṟi avar muṭivāyc colluva tāṉatu anēkamāyp piḻiyillāmal cariyākavēyirukkiṟatu eṉṟu ellārum oppukkoḷvārkaḷ. 
“piṉvarum pakkaṅkaḷil collappaṭṭa pala ārōkaṇaṅkaḷi yum inta vāttiyattil (curuti ārmōṉiyam) tēvāl eṉpavaruṭiya tayaviṉālum aptul karīm mutaliya pāṭṭu vittuvāṉkaḷiṉ utaviyik koṇṭum cōtittu niccayit tirukkiṟārkaḷ. aṭiyil varum aṭṭavaṇiyāṉatu atikamāy upayōkattilirukkappaṭṭa 24 curutikaḷ eviyeṉṟum avi kaḷil evikaḷ intiya ārmōṉiyattil varukiṟateṉṟum, avikaḷukkum paḻiya curutikaḷukkum irukkum tāratam miyam eṉṉaveṉṟum kāṭṭukiṟatu.”
mēlē kaṇṭa vākkiyaṅkaḷil tām intustāṉi caṅkīta vittuvāṉ aptul karīmik koṇṭum tēvāliṉ ārāycciyil kaṇṭupiṭitta curaṅkaḷik koṇṭum curutikaḷip paṟṟip piṉ varum aṭṭavaṇi tayār ceytatākac collukiṟār.














4-vatu aṭṭavaṇi.
intiyāviluḷḷa intustāṉi caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷ iṉṉavi yeṉṟu
E.kiḷameṇṭs avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyattik kāṭṭum
curutiyiṉ aṭṭavaṇi.
* ivvaṭiyāḷamuḷḷa kaṇakkukaḷ innūlukkuriyavi
			
		kuṟippu : inta aṭṭavaṇi muṟṟilum tēvāl avarkaḷiṉ pustakattil 29-vatu pakkattiluḷḷa D aṭṭavaṇiyākavēyirukkiṟatu. eṉṟālum tēvāl eṭuttuk koṇṭa 10-vatu curutikkup piṉ oru curutiyum, 17-vatu curutikkup piṉ oru curutiyum, nūtaṉamāyc colluvatē itil kavaṉikkappaṭa vēṇṭiyatu. pattāvatu curutikku 320 ōciyiṉ alikaḷum 11-vatu curutikku 3371/2 ōciyiṉ alikaḷu mirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu tēvāl collukiṟār. ati kiḷameṇṭs oppuk koṇṭu 320 ōciyiṉ alikaḷukkuppiṉ 324 ōciyiṉ alikaḷiyuṭiya oru curutiyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu collukiṟār. appaṭiyē tēvāl colliya 17-vatu curuti 405 ōciyiṉ alikaḷi yuṭiyateṉṟum 18-vatu curuti 4262/3 ōciyiṉ alikaḷiyuṭiya teṉṟum oppukkoṇṭu, itaṉ naṭumattiyil 405kkup piṟaku 420 ōciyiṉ alikaḷiyuṭiya oru curutiyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum collukiṟār. ivviraṇṭu curutikaḷum aptul karīmāl pāṭappaṭṭu mikunta iṉimi yuṭiyatākat tam aṉupavattil kaṇṭa curaṅkaḷ eṉṟum collukiṟār. ivviraṇṭu nuṭpamāṉa curaṅkaḷiyum tam kātiṉāl kēṭṭu aṟintu koṇṭatum tāṉ kēṭṭati evvita cantēkamumiṉṟi maṟṟavarkaḷukkuc colla niṉittatum mikavum mēlāṉatē. oru mēṟṟici caṅkīta vittuvāṉ intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷi ārāyvatum, avikaḷ iṉṉatu tāṉ eṉṟu niccayippatum ilakuvāṉa kāriyamalla. ivviraṇṭu curutikaḷ mattimattiṟku mēl oṉṟum, tivatattiṟku mēloṉṟumāka iruppatu pōla maṟṟa curaṅkaḷukku naṭumattiyilum ivaṟṟip pōlavum ivaṟṟiṟkuc ciṟitākavum anēkam vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu nam intiya tēcattuc caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷil evarum kaṇitappaṭi rucuppaṭuttik kāṭṭak kūṭiyavarkaḷ alla. āṉāl paṭittukkāṭṭa māttiram kūṭiyavarkaḷāyirukkiṟārkaḷ. itaṉālēyē intiya caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷukkuḷ curuti viṣayamāy oṟṟumi yuṇṭākavilli. 320kkum 3371/2kkum, 405kkum 4262/3kkum uḷḷa iṭiveḷikaḷip pōlavē, 4555/8kkum 480kkum naṭuvilum, 432kkum 450kkum, 384kkum 400kkum naṭuvilum, 360kkum 378kkum naṭuvilum, 3441/3kkum 360kkum naṭuvilum, 288kkum 300kkum naṭuvilum 270kkum 2844/9kkum naṭuvilum, 240kkum 252kkum naṭuvilum pōla iṉṉum ettaṉiyō iṭaṅkaḷil nuṭpamāṉa curutikaḷ varalām. 320kkup piṉ 324l oru curuti pēcukiṟateṉṟāl, 328l oṉṟum 332l oṉṟum ēṉ pēcamāṭṭātu? appaṭiyē 4262/3l oru curutiyum 420l oru curutiyum pēcumāṉāl, 414l oṉṟum 408l oṉṟum ēṉ pēcakkūṭātu? ippaṭi yiruppataṉāl mikunta oṟṟumiyuṭiyatum iṉimiyuṭiyatumāṉa anēka curutikaḷ intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu nām aṟiya vēṇṭum. avikaḷiyellām piṉvarum teṉ intiya caṅkītattiṉ curutikaḷip paṟṟic collum aṭṭavaṇiyil kāṇalām.
mēṟṟiciyār curaṅkaḷukkuc collum ōciyiṉ alikaḷi ottirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu tēvāl ceyta tiruttaṅkaḷukku upayōkitta kaṇakkukaḷ cariyallaveṉṟu coṉṉatu pōlavē itaṟkum colla vēṇṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu.
21-vatu curutikkut tēvāl 4555/8 eṉṟu collukiṟār. āṉāl kiḷameṇṭs 4551/9 eṉṟu collukiṟār. itu tāṅkaḷ eṭuttukkoṇṭa piṉṉaṅkaḷiṉāl vanta coṟpak kuṟivu. itu oṉṟu tavira, maṟṟa enta eṇkaḷilum tēvāli ottirukkiṟār.











aintāvatu.
caṅkīta ratṉākarar muṟippaṭi kirāmam māṟṟukiyil nūtaṉamākak kiṭikkiṟateṉṟu
E.kiḷameṇṭs avarkaḷ collum 3 curutikaḷ. 
caṅkīta ratṉākaram 3-vatu pirakaraṇam 24 mutaṟ koṇṭu 38 varikkumuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷiṉpaṭi ceyyappaṭṭatu.
inta aṭṭavaṇiyil 3 curutikaḷi 22 curutikaḷōṭu cērttuc colli yirukkiṟār. 22 curutikaḷi ippaṭic ceyya vēṇṭumeṉṟu coṉṉa caṅkīta ratṉākararē ṣaṭjama kirāmam, mattima kirāmam, kāntāra kirāmam eṉṉum mūṉṟu kirāmattil curutikaḷi māṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu colliyirukkiṟatiliruntu inta nūtaṉamāṉa 3 curutikaḷum kiṭittaṉa eṉṟu collukiṟār. atāvatu 300 ōciyiṉ alikaḷiyuṭiya antara kāntārattukkuk kīḻ 2968/12 ōciyiṉ alikaḷiyuṭiya oru curutiyum 360 ōciyiṉ alikaḷiyuṭiya pañcamattiṉ kīḻ 3555/9 ōciyiṉ ali kaḷiyuṭiya oru curutiyum varukiṟateṉṟum 450 ōciyiṉ ali kaḷiyuṭiya kākali niṣātattiṟkuk kīḻ 4441/9 ōciyiṉ ali kaḷiyuṭiya oru curutiyum varavēṇṭumeṉṟum collukiṟār. tēvāl avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyattil, mutal curuti 252 ōciyiṉ alikaḷukkup patil 250 eṉṟum oṉpatāvatil 315 ōciyiṉ alikaḷukkup patil 3161/8 eṉṟum, 12vatil 3411/3 kkup patil 3453/5 eṉṟum, 14vatil 378kkup patil 375 eṉṟum nālu iṭaṅkaḷil coṟpa vittiyācam collukiṟārēyoḻiya maṟṟa ellā viṭaṅkaḷilum, avaruṭiya apippirāyattit taḻuviyē eḻuti yirukkiṟār. caṅkīta ratṉākararuṭiya apippirāyattiṉpaṭi ṣaṭjama mattima kāntāra kirāmaṅkaḷ vittukkoṇṭu pōkuṅ kālattil 22 curutikaḷit tavira vēṟu enta curutikaḷum piṟakkamāṭṭā. ari ari curaṅkaḷāy aminta oru stāyiyil oru curam māṟṟik koṇṭu mēl pōvataṉāl atil irukkum curaṅkaḷtāṉ piṟakkumēyallāmal naṭuvil vēṟu oru curam piṟakkamāṭṭātu. atupōlavē 22 curutikaḷuḷḷa stāyikaḷil ṣaṭjama mattima kāntāra kirāmaṅkaḷ māṟṟuvataṉāl 22 curutiyiṉ variciyil varumēyoḻiya vēṟu varici uṇṭākamāṭṭātu. kirāmam eṉpatu ovvoru curamum ittaṉi ittaṉi curutiyōṭu varavēṇṭumeṉṟu amittuk koṇṭu ārōkaṇa avarōkaṇa vitippaṭi kāṉam ceyvatām. kirāmattiṟkāka curutikaḷi eppaṭi māṟṟik koṇṭālum, mottattil ellā curutikaḷum cērntu 22 tāṉirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu potuvāy amintirukkiṟatu. ivaṟṟil ṣaṭjama kirāmamāvatu ṣaṭjamam 4, riṣapam 3, kāntāram 2, mattimam 4, pañcamam 4, tivatam 3, niṣātam 2 eṉṟu 22 curutikaḷ varuvatām. ivi muṟiyē ṣaṭjamattiṉ 4, 3, 2, 1 eṉṟa curutikaḷil ārampittu muṭivaṭiyum. mattima kirāmam ṣaṭjamam 4, riṣapam 3, kāntāram 2, mattimam 4, pañcamam 3, tivatam 4, niṣātam 2 eṉṉum 22 curutikaḷōṭu varuvatu. ituvum muṉpōlavē ṣaṭjamattiṉ 4, 3, 2, 1 eṉṉum curutikaḷiṉ muṟiyē ārampittu sapta cura kiramappaṭi kāṉam ceyyappaṭum. kāntāra kirāmamāvatu ṣaṭjamam 4, riṣapam 2, kāntāram 4, mattimam 3, pañcamam 3, tivatam 3, niṣātam 3 āka 22 curutikaḷiyumuṭiya sapta curaṅkaḷ muṟiyē ṣaṭjamattiṉ 4, 3, 2, 1 eṉṉum curutikaḷil ārampittuk kāṉam ceyyappaṭuvateṉṟē colla vēṇṭum.













5-vatu aṭṭavaṇi.
intustāṉi caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷ iṉṉaviyeṉṟu
E..kiḷameṇṭs avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyattik kāṭṭum curutiyiṉ aṭṭavaṇi.
caṅkīta ratṉākara muṟippaṭi ṣaṭja, mattima, kāntāra kirāmaṅkaḷi māṟṟumpōtu, kūṭutalākak kiṭikkum mūṉṟu curutikaḷ.
* ivvaṭiyāḷamuḷḷa kaṇakkukaḷ innūlukkuriyavi
kiraka māṟṟum viṣayattippaṟṟi mēṟcolliya cila vacaṉaṅkaḷi aṭiyil varum aṭṭavaṇi mikat teḷivākak kāṭṭum.
* ṣaṭjakirāmam.
caṅkīta ratṉākarar colliya muṟippaṭi

* mattimakirāmam.
caṅkīta ratṉākarar colliya muṟippaṭi

* kāntārakirāmam
caṅkīta ratṉākarar colliya muṟippaṭi

tiruṣṭāntamāka ṣaṭja kirāmattil ṣaṭjamattiṉ nālu curutikaḷi yum ovvoṉṟāka iṭatupakkam taḷḷikkoṇṭu pōkiyil mēl variciyiṉ ṣaṭjamattiṉ iraṇṭām curutiyil ṣaṭjamattiṉ nālu curuti vittu ārampippōmāṉāl iraṇṭa curutikaḷiyuṭiya kāntāramum niṣātamum riṣapa tivataṅkaḷil layam aṭikiṉṟaṉa. atāvatu riṣapa tivatattiṉ mūṉṟām curutiyē kāntāramākavum niṣātamākavum ākiṟatu. ippaṭiyē ṣaṭjamattiṉ mutal curutiyil ṣaṭjamattiṉ nālām curutiyi vittu ārampikkumpoḻutu mūṉṟu curutikaḷuṭiya riṣapamum tivatamum ṣaṭjama pañcamaṅkaḷil layam aṭikiṉṟaṉa. atāvatu ṣaṭjamam riṣapamākavum, pañcamam tivatamākavum varukiṟatu. irupattiraṇṭām curutiyākiya niṣātattil nālām curuti ārampikkum pōtu niṣātattil ṣaṭjamamum kāntārat til mattimamum mattimattil pañcamum pēcukiṟatu. appōtu tivatattiṉ 18, 19, 20 eṉṟa mūṉṟu curutikaḷil iraṇṭāvatākiya pattoṉpatāvatu curutiyil mutalāvatu ṣaṭjam ārampikkiṟatu. itupōlavē ovvoru kirāmamum taṉ taṉ curattiṉ eṇkaḷōṭum aḷavōṭum kiraman tavaṟāmal naṭipeṟum. Mr. kiḷameṇṭs avarkaḷiṉ curutik kaṇakkiṉ aḷaviyum kaṇakkiyum itaṉpiṉ aṭṭavaṇiyākak koṭuttirukkiṟōm. anta aḷavi ari aṅkula akalamuḷḷa nīḷamāṉa nālu tuṇṭu kaṭitaṅkaḷil kuṟittuk koṇṭu oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāyk kirakam māṟṟi vittup pārppā mēyāṉāl avikaḷ ērāḷamāṉa iṭiveḷikaḷiyum curaṅkaḷiyu muṇṭākkumeṉpati pirattiyaṭcamāy aṟiyalām.
mēlum itaṉ muṉṉuḷḷa aintāvatu aṭṭavaṇi oṉpatāvatu kalattil curutikaḷiṉ iṭiveḷikaḷi ceṉṭs kaṇakkil colli yiruppatik kavaṉittāl 22, 41, 49, 71, 90 ceṉṭskaḷ eṉṟu aintu pētattil collukiṟār. āṉāl nālāvatu aṭṭavaṇiyil 20, 22, 27, 63, 71, 84, 90, 92 ceṉṭskaḷāṉa eṭṭupētamāṉa aḷavukaḷ colliyirukkiṟār. ivvaḷavu pētamāṉa aḷavukaḷ, cāraṅkar muṟippaṭi curuti māṟṟum poḻutu, evvitattilum ottuvarāta ērāḷamāṉa curutikaḷit tarum eṉṟu aṟivāḷikaḷ kavaṉippārkaḷ. mūṉṟu curaṅkaḷ kirakamāṟṟumpōtu uṇṭākiṟateṉṟu coṉṉatu poruntātatāyiruntālum pāṭakarkaḷik koṇṭu maṟṟavarkaḷ 22 eṉṟu collum curaṅkaḷukkumēl 3 curaṅkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittu niccayittuk kuṟittatāṉatu nām meccik koḷḷak kūṭiyatu.
caṅkīta ratṉākarattil kaṇṭa immūṉṟu kirāmaṅkaḷit tavira vēṟu kirāmamum paḻamiyāṉa tamiḻ nūlākiya cilappatikārattil kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu. atil marutayāḻ, kuṟiñciyāḻ, neytalyāḻ, pāliyāḻ eṉṟa 4 kirāmaṅkaḷ iruntatākat terikiṟatu. ivikaḷil marutayāḻ kural allatu ṣaṭjamam, 4 tuttam allatu riṣapam 4, kikkiḷi allatu kāntāram 3, uḻi allatu mattimam 2, iḷi allatu pañcamam 4, viḷari allatu tivatam 3, tāram allatu niṣātam 2 āka 22 curutikaḷum 22 alakākac collappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itik kavaṉikkiyil ṣaṭja mattima kāntāra kirāmameṉṟu caṅkīta ratṉākarar collum mūṉṟu vitamāṉa muṟikaḷukkum itu vēṟāṉa muṟi eṉpatākat terikiṟatu. itit tavira, āyappāli, tirikōṇappāli, caturappāliyeṉṉum mūṉṟu muṟikaḷukkum alaku collappaṭavilli. ivikaḷi naṉṟāyk kavaṉikkiyil oru stāyiyil 22 curutikaḷi ēḻucuraṅkaḷukkum pākittu, iṉṉa iṉṉa aḷavil ēḻu curaṅkaḷ varavēṇṭu meṉṟu coṉṉatēyoḻiya vēṟilli. inta 22 curutikaḷuḷ kirakam māṟṟuvataṉāl ṣaṭjamattiṉ curutikaḷ nālum, muṟiyē ovvoṉṟāy iṭam pērntu vaḻaṅkukiṟa muṟi oṉṟum ṣaṭjamattiṉ nālāvatu curuti yiliruntu valam pērntupōkiṟa muṟiyoṉṟumāka iraṇṭu muṟi iruntatākat terikiṟatu. ivikaḷil valam pērntu varum muṟi caṅkīta ratṉākarattil kāṇappaṭavilli. āṉāl mikappaḻamiyāṉa tamiḻ nūl kaḷil collappaṭukiṟatu. iṉṉum maṟṟa vivaraṅkaḷ yāviyum piṉṉāl varum aṭṭavaṇiyil kaṇṭu koḷka.
Mr. kiḷameṇṭs avarkaḷ caṅkīta ratṉākararuṭiya tuvāvimcati curutikaḷiṉpaṭi intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷ eṉṟu colliyirukkum aṭṭavaṇiyil 15 curaṅkaḷ iṅkilīṣ eṉ ārmāṉik skēli (Enharmonic Scale) liruntu eṭukkappaṭṭavi. maṟṟum 10 curaṅkaḷil 3 curutikaḷ kirāmam māṟuvatiṉāl uṇṭāṉaviyeṉṟum mītiyāṉa 7 curam 25 curutiyic cērntateṉṟum collukiṟār.
mottamāka Mr kiḷameṇṭs eḻutiya pustakattip pārkkiyil tēvāl avarkaḷuṭiya curuti muṟiyiṉāl vaḻi tavaṟip pōyirukkalām eṉpatēyoḻiya maṟṟappaṭi avaruṭiya nuṭpamāṉa vicāraṇikkāka nāṉ mikavum cantōṣappaṭukiṟēṉ. aptul karīm mutaliya caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷ pāṭit tām kēṭṭatil ivikaḷ intustāṉi rākattiṟku varukiṟateṉṟu colluvati nāṉ oppuk koḷkiṟēṉ. vaḻakkattil muṟṟilum ēṟukku māṟāṉa anēka intustāṉi muṟikaḷippōl ituvum intiya caṅkītattiṟku vittiyācamākayirukka vēṇṭum. āṉāl mottamāy oru stāyikkuḷ curutikaḷi eṅkē eṭuttukkoṇṭu kāṉam paṇṇiṉālum avikaḷukku oru oḻuṅkāṉamuṟi irukka vēṇṭiyatu ciramameṉṟu nāṉ niṉikkiṟēṉ. caṅkīta ratṉākarar camaskirutattil eḻutiyavaṟṟi vācikkāmalum, vācittālum arttam teriyāmalum, vārttiyiṉ aḷavāka arttam terintālum uḷporuḷ aṟiyāmalum, uḷporuḷ terintum cātaṉikkuk koṇṭu varāmalum, ēmāntuniṟkum intiya vittuva cirōmaṇikaḷ ettaṉiyō pērkaḷirukka, Mr. kiḷameṇṭs avarkaḷ tuvāvimcati curutikaḷippaṟṟi vicārikkavum vicārittavi kaḷil muṭivāṉa vikaḷip piṟarukkut terivikkavum muṉ vantatu mikavum mēṉmi yāṉatē.
intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷukkut takunta vitamāy ārmōṉiyam ceyvatu kūṭātakāriyamāyiruntālum, cila curutikaḷi amittu ārmōṉiyattil colluvatāṉatu, itupōl iṉṉum anēka curutikaḷ varalāmeṉṟu niccayippataṟku oru mutaṟpaṭi pōlirukkiṟatu eṉṟu pukaṭinantu collak kūṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu.
maṟṟum ivaruṭiya curutimuṟiyil kavaṉikkavēṇṭiya mukkiya kuṟippukaḷi nām iṅku collāmal viṭṭālum aṭṭavaṇiyil teḷivākak kāṇalām. 



āṟāvatu.
III. cāraṅkar curuti muṟippaṭi teṉṉintiya caṅkītam irukkiṟateṉṟu collum
mūṉṟām vakuppār.
Retired Inspector of Schools  makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi rāv pakatūr
C. nākōji rāv avarkaḷiṉ 22 curutiyiṉ muṟi.
intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum 22 curutikaḷip paṟṟi makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi nākōjirāv avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyam iṅkilīṣil eḻutap paṭṭut tañcāvūr Trining School 1st Assistant  makā-rācarāca- ciṟi cuntaramiyar B.A.L.T. avarkaḷāl tamiḻil moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭu, tañci caṅkīta vittiyā makājaṉa caṅkattiṉ 2-vatu kāṉperaṉsil paṭikkappaṭṭatu.
iṅku avviyācattiṉ cila mukkiya pākaṅkaḷip pārppōm :-
caṅkīta vittiyā makājaṉa caṅkam 2-vatu kāṉpareṉs rippōrṭ pakkam 53-66.
“namatu caṅkītattil oru stāyiyil 12 curastāṉaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu caṅkītavittuvāṉkaḷ collukiṟārkaḷ, intap paṉṉiraṇṭum, (sa),cutta riṣapam (ri), catur curuti riṣapam (ri), allatu cutta kāntāram (ka), sātāraṇa kāntāram (ka), allatu ṣaṭcuruti riṣapam (ri), antarakāntāram (ka), cutta mattiyamam (ma), piratimattiyamam (ma), pa, cutta tivatam (ta), catur curuti tivatam (ta), allatu cuttaniṣātam (ni), kicika niṣātam (ni), allatu ṣaṭ curuti tivatam (ta) kākali niṣātam (ni) ākiya ippaṉṉiraṇṭumām. itiyē airōppiya caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷ aintu muḻuc curaṅkaḷum ēḻu aric curaṅkaḷum ākap paṉṉiraṇṭu curastāṉaṅkaḷ eṉṟu collu kiṟārkaḷ. 
mēlē coṉṉa curapētaṅkaḷit tavira, iṉṉum ciṟu pētaṅ kaḷuḷḷa curaṅkaḷirukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum avi namatu caṅkītattil varukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum collukiṟārkaḷ. cilar, airōppiyaratu caṅkītattil illāta 1/4 curaṅkaḷum iṉṉum ciṟiya curaṅkaḷum varukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu eḻutukiṟārkaḷ. mēṟcoṉṉa 12 curaṅkaḷil, orē vaki ārōkaṇa avarōkaṇa curaṅkaḷuḷḷaviyāyiruntu pāṭiṉāl kātil vēṟu vēṟu rākaṅkaḷākap pulappaṭum rākaṅ kaḷip paṟṟik kēṭṭāl, namatu caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷ, antanta rākaṅkaḷil kamaka pētaṅkaḷiruppatāl vēṟuvēṟu rākaṅkaḷāy viṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu vivaramillāta oru maṟumoḻi colli viṭukiṟārkaḷ. namatu cāstira pustakaṅkaḷil, oru stāyiyil iruppatu 22 curaṅkaḷ allatu curutikaḷ eṉṟu viyaktamāy eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. intak kāraṇaṅkaḷi yellām koṇṭu nām muṭivu ceyya vēṇṭiyateṉṉa veṉṟāl, namatu caṅkītattil iruppatu 12 curaṅkaḷ alla, ataṟku mēṟpaṭṭuttāṉ irukka vēṇṭumeṉpatē. 
iṉi nām aṟiyavēṇṭiyateṉṉaveṉṟāl oru stāyi yil niccayamāy ettaṉi curaṅkaḷ varukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum avi eṉṉaveṉṉa eṉpatumām. 
nām ārāyntu pārttamaṭṭil, namatu caṅkītattil oru stāyi yil namatu cāstirap pustakaṅkaḷiṟ colliyatu pōla 22 curaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu terikiṟatu. mēlum inta 22 curaṅ kaḷit tavira vēṟu curaṅkaḷ varamuṭiyāteṉṟum terikiṟatu. 
airōppiya caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷ oru curam eṉpatu antac curam cattikkum ātāramāṉa vastu etuvō atu naṭuṅkuvatāltāṉ uṇṭākiṟateṉṟu collukiṟārkaḷ. mēlum kuṟippiṭṭa nēra moṉṟil anta naṭuṅkum vastu atikamāṉa taṭavi naṭuṅkiṉāl mēl curamum kuṟinta taṭavi naṭuṅkiṉāl tāṭi nanta curamum uṇṭākum eṉṟum kaṇṭaṟintirukkiṟārkaḷ. mēlum ca eṉpatu evvaḷavu vēpaṉaṅkaḷāl (Vibration- vēpaṉam) allatu ōciyalikaḷāl uṇṭākiṟatō ativiṭa iraṭṭippu maṭaṅku vēpaṉaṅkaḷāl mēl stāyi ca uṇṭākiṟateṉṟum kaṇṭaṟintiruk kiṟārkaḷ. 
antak kaṇakkuppirakāram pārttatil oru stāyiyil piṉ varukiṟa 22 curaṅkaḷum varum.

inta variciyil ovvoru curattiṟkum etiril eḻuti yirukkum kaṇakku antac curam (1)ca eṉpatum ettaṉi vēpaṉaṅ kaḷāl āṉatō ativiṭa ittaṉi maṭaṅku vēpaṉaṅkaḷāl āṉatu eṉpatik kuṟikkiṟatu. ca eṉpatu 240 vēpaṉaṅkaḷāl āṉāl, ri (1) (mutal riṣapam) 250 vēpaṉaṅkaḷāl uṇṭākiṟatu. ri (2) (iraṇṭāvatu riṣapam) 256 vēpaṉaṅkaḷāl ākiṟatu. atē mātiri kaṇakkiṭṭup pārttuk koḷḷavum. 
ippaṭi 22 curaṅkaḷtāṉ irukkiṉṟaṉa. ataṟkumēl irukka muṭiyātu eṉpataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ iṉṉavitāṉ eṉṟu mutalil eḻutukiṟēṉ. 
airōppiyaratu saṅkītattil, sa, ka(3), pa, sa1, ākiya inta nālu svaraṅkaḷum cērntu captittāl iṉṉavitāṉ eṉṟu mutalil eḻutukiṟēṉ. 
airōppiyaratu saṅkītattil, sa, ka(3), pa, sa, ākiya inta nālu svaraṅkaḷum cērntu captittāl kātukkuc cukattit tarukiṉṟaṉa. inta nālu svaraṅkaḷukkum “cērum svaraṅkaḷ” eṉṟu peyar vaḻaṅkukiṟārkaḷ. ippaṭi cērntu svaraṅkaḷ captippatiṉāl  uṇṭākum cukattukku hārmani (harmony) eṉṟu peyar. airōppiyaratu saṅkītam muḻuvatum ippaṭi hārmaniyē niṟintatām. 
ippaṭi cukam uṇṭāvataṟkuk kāraṇam uṇṭu. ateṉṉa veṉṟāl, oru tanti vāttiyattil sa eṉṉum captam uṇṭākumpaṭi oru tantiyi mīṭṭiṉāl, ataṉuṭaṉē atē kālattil ka(3), pa, sa1, ākiya immūṉṟu svaraṅkaḷum uṇṭāki nam kātil viḻukiṉṟaṉa. (mēlum oru piṭil tantiyil villip pōṭṭuk koṇṭu iṭatu ki viralāl tantiyit toṭṭatum toṭātatu māka virali mēlē iḻuttuk koṇṭē pōṉāl sa, ka(3), pa, sa1 inta nālu svaraṅkaḷ maṭṭil nam kātil viḻukiṉṟaṉavē yoḻiya vēṟu svaraṅkaḷ nam kātil viḻukiṟatilli.) eṉavē, namatu kātu sa eṉpatu captikka kēṭṭavuṭaṉē ka(3), pa, sa1 inta svaraṅkaḷiyum kēṭka cittamākiviṭukiṟatu. anta svaraṅkaḷ antac camayattil kātil viḻuntāl cukatti uṇṭākku kiṉṟaṉa. intac cukattukku hārmani eṉṟu peyar. inta svaraṅkaḷukku svapāvattil cērum svaraṅkaḷ (Chord of nature) kārṭ āp nēccar eṉṟu peyar. atē mātiriyāy, inta svaraṅkaḷ oṉṟaṉpiṉṉoṉṟāy anukkiramamāka vantāl anta cukattukku melaṭi (melody) allatu (namatu saṅkītattil uḷḷa) rākam eṉṟa peyar vaḻakkattil irukkiṟatu. appaṭi cukam taruvataṟkuk kāraṇamum, muṉ coṉṉa mātiri inta svaraṅkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu cērntu vantāl hārmaniyit taruvatutāṉ. 
sa, ka(3), pa, sa1 eṉṉum nālu svaraṅkaḷiyum eṭuttuk koṇṭāl sa1 eṉṉum svarattiṟkum sa eṉṉum svarat tiṟkum iṭiveḷi 2 ākiṟatu. atāvatu sa1 eṉpatu sa-vi viṭa iraṇṭu maṭaṅku vēpanaṅkaḷāl āṉātu. (inta iraṇṭu svaramum atika oṟṟumiyuḷḷaṉavātalāl cērntu vantāl nalla hārmaniyit taruvataṉālum, oṉṟōṭoṉṟu naṉṟāy iḻintu koṇṭu taṉittaṉi svaraṅkaḷākak kātukkup pulappaṭātirukkiṟataṉālum inta iraṇṭu svaraṅ kaḷukkum orē peyar vittirukkiṟārkaḷ) ākavē nalla iṭiveḷikaḷil 2-m oṉṟām. 
piṟaku, pa eṉpataṟkum sa eṉpataṟkum iṭiveḷi 3/2. atāvatu pa, sa-vi viṭa 3/2 allatu 11/2 maṭaṅku vēpanaṅkaḷāl āṉatu. inta iraṇṭu svaraṅkaḷum naṉṟāyc cērakkūṭiya svaraṅkaḷtāṉ. āṉāl sa’-vum sa-vum evvaḷavu naṉṟāyc cērumō avvaḷavu naṉṟāy avikaḷ cērukiṟatilli. ākiyiṉāl 3/2 eṉpatu iṉṉoru nalla iṭiveḷiyākiṟatu. (inta mātiri, sa, pa, sa’ immūṉṟum oṉṟōṭoṉṟu naṉṟāy cērvatu paṟṟi, vīṇi mutaliya vāttiyaṅkaḷil sa, pa, sa’ eṉṟu karuti vittuk koḷḷukiṟārkaḷ.) 
itē mātiri kaṇakkiṭṭup pārttāl sa-vukkum pa-vukkum iṭiveḷi 4/3 ākiṟatu. atu piṉ varumāṟu :-
pa x 4/3 = sa’
3/2 x 4/3 = 2 
ka(3)-vukkum sa-vukkum iṭiveḷi 5/4 ākiṟatu. pa-vukkum ka(3)-vukkum iṭiveḷi 6/5 ākiṟatu. atu piṉ varumāṟu : 
ka(3) x 6/5 = pa. 
5/4 x 6/5 = 3/2 
mēlē coṉṉatiliruntu nām muḻutum aṟintu koṇṭa teṉṉaveṉṟāl :- 
2 allatu 2/1, 3/2, 4/3, 5/4, 6/5 
inta iṭiveḷikaḷ nalla iṭiveḷikaḷ. inta iṭiveḷi kaḷuḷḷa svaraṅkaḷ anukkiramamāka vantāl rākam naṉṟāy uṇṭākum eṉpatutāṉ. 
mēlum, itiviṭa vēṟu cila ciṟiya iṭiveḷikaḷuḷḷa svaraṅkaḷ cērntālum cukam tarumeṉṟu airōppiya saṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷ apippirāyam koḷḷukiṟārkaḷ. avikaḷāvaṉa:-
(1) sa-vukku 9/8 maṭaṅku vēpanaṅkaḷāl uṇṭākum mējar ṭōṉ (major tone)  allatu periya svaram.
(2) sa-vukku 10/9 maṭaṅku vēpanaṅkaḷāl uṇṭākum minar ṭōṉ (minor tone) allatu ciṉṉa svaram.
(3) sa-vukku 16/15 maṭaṅku vēpanaṅkaḷāl uṇṭākum mējar semiṭōṉ (major semitone) allatu periya ari svaram.
 (4) sa-vukku 25/24 maṭaṅku vēpanaṅkaḷāl uṇṭākum minar semiṭōṉ (minor semitone)  allatu ciṉṉa ari svaram ākiya ivikaḷām.
antak kāraṇattikkoṇṭē mēlē coṉṉa iṭiveḷi kaḷuḷḷa svaraṅkaḷ anukkiramamāka vantālum melaṭi (melody)  allatu rākam eṉṉum cukattit tarum eṉṟum nām aṟintu koḷḷalām. ēṉeṉil, cila svaraṅkaḷ cērntāl hārmani uṇṭākum pakṣattil avikaḷ anukkiramamāy vantāl melaṭi uṇṭākum eṉṟu ūkikkalāmallavā?
itiliruntu nām kāṇpatu eṉṉaveṉṟāl 25/24, 16/15, 10/9, 9/8, 6/5, 5/4 inta iṭiveḷikaḷuḷḷa svaraṅkaḷ vantāl cukattit tarum eṉpatē.
sa, pa, sa1 immūṉṟu svaraṅkaḷum cērntu vantāl hārmani irukkiṟateṉṟu vitvāṉkaḷ yārum oppuk koḷḷukiṟapaṭi yāl avikaḷuḷḷa iṭiveḷikaḷum nalla iṭiveḷi kaḷākum. avi 4/5, 3/2, 2 āka im mūṉṟumām.
1. nām iṉi kavaṉikkavēṇṭiyateṉṉaveṉṟāl, sa eṉpataṟ kum ka(3) eṉpataṟkum iṭiyil cērumpaṭiyāṉa iṭiveḷi kaḷuḷḷa enta enta svaraṅkaḷ varalām eṉṟu pārkka vēṇṭiyatutāṉ. atē mātiri ka(3) vukkum, pa vukkum, pa vukkum sa1 vukkum iṭiyil enta svaraṅkaḷ varalām eṉṟu pārkka vēṇṭum.
mutalil sa eṉpataṟkum ka(3) vukkum iṭiyil ri (1) eṉṉum svaram vantāl naṉṟāyc cērum. ēṉeṉṟāl ri (1) kkum sa vukkum iṭiveḷi 25/24 ka(3) vukkum ri (1) kkum iṭiveḷi 6/5, kāraṇam:-
25/24 x 6/5 = 5/4
ri (1) x 6/5 = ka(3).
atāvatu ri(1) yic curutiyāka vittuk koṇṭāl ka(3) eṉpatu anta curutikku sātāraṇa kāntāramākap pēcum. piṟaku, ri (3) eṉṉum svaramum sa vukkum ka(3) vukkum iṭiyil vantāl iraṇṭuṭaṉum naṉṟāyc cērum. ēṉeṉṟāl (ri3) kkum sa vukkum iṭiveḷi 10/9 ka(3) vukkum ri(3) kkum iṭiveḷi 9/8. itaṟkuk kāraṇam :-
10/9 x 9/8 = 5/4
ri(3) x 9/8 = ka(3).
atāvatu ri(3) yic curutiyāy vittuk koṇṭāl, ka(3) ataṟku ri(4) ākap pēcum.
piṟaku ri(4), sa vukkum, ka(3) vukkum iṭiyil vantāl cukattit tarum. ēṉeṉṟāl ri (4)kkum sa vukkum iṭiveḷi 9/8. ka(3) vukkum ri(4)kkum iṭiveḷi 10/9. itaṟkuk kāraṇam :-
9/8 x 10/9 = 5/4
ri(4) x 10/9 = ka(3)
atāvatu ri(4) eṉpatic curutiyāy vittuk koṇṭāl ataṟku ka(3), ri(3) āyp pēcum.
6/5, 9/8, 10/9 inta iṭiveḷikaḷ mēlē eḻutiyirukkum iṭiveḷikaḷuḷ oṉṟākum eṉkiṟatiyum kāṇka.
ka(2) (allatu, cila cila rākaṅkaḷil varum ṣaṭcuruti riṣapam) sa vukkum ka(3) vukkum naṭuvil vantāl cērntu varum. ka(2) vukkum sa vukkum iṭiveḷi 6/5. ka(3) vukkum ka(2) vukkum iṭiveḷi 25/24. itaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa veṉṟāl :-
6/5 x 25/24 = 5/4
ka(2) x 25/24 = ka(3).
atāvatu ka(2) vic curutiyāy vittuk koṇṭāl ka(3) ataṟku ri(4) āyp pēcum.
ākavē, piṉ varum svara varicikaḷ cērumpaṭiyāṉavi.
sa ri(1) 	ka(3)
sa ri(3)	 ka(3)
sa ri(4) 	ka(3)
sa sari(6) = ka(2)] ka(3)
itē mātiri kaṇakkiṭṭup pārttāl, ka(3) vukkum pa vukkum iṭiyil ententa svaraṅkaḷ varum eṉṟu pārppōm.

5/4 x 16/15 = 4/3
ka(3) x 16/15 = ma(1)
